| -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Minimum data**                 | For the account: the role row and `player_agreed_at`. For a room: who was in it and when (`room_players`; with `seen_ms`, when their room page last read a running game's state, which only tells a round whom to wait for and is never shown), and each answer — the items picked, right or wrong, the time, the points (`room_answers`); from T2 each taunt — who threw which key at whom and when (`room_taunts`), the target emptied after a day. No age, date of birth, age group, gender, phone, photo, avatar, nickname, place, device id. From C3 (ADR 031), for the voice: with the account, `voice_agreed_at`; with the room, `voice_at` and `voice_epoch`; with a player's row, `voice_admitted_at` and `voice_muted_at`. Nothing said, and not who joined the voice, whose microphone was open or who spoke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| **Nothing typed**                | A room has no name and no chat; a player never types an answer, and a taunt (T2) is a key of our own list, never text. No free text of a person is stored for a room, so nothing needs moderating and nothing typed can reach a log.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| **Who sees a name**              | The host and the players of a room the account is or was in: on the room page, the screen and the results. Nobody else, and never an e-mail address or an account id. There is no list of rooms or players, no search and no profile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| **Never on the wire or in logs** | A player's name is never in a log, an exception, a broadcast or a URL; a device knows a player by `room_players.id`. Names reach a page only in authenticated HTTP: the room page's props and the state — and, from ADR 030, the winner's name clip, which only the host's screen gets, by ids (`/rooms/{room}/members/{member}/voice`), never by a name or a hash, kept by no cache. No room page makes a third-party request. The voice server knows a player by `room_players.id` too and is never given a name; a ticket is never in a log, a redirect or a broadcast; the voice passes only through the instance's own voice server, and no public STUN server is asked (ADR 031 decision 2).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| **No contact**                   | No message, chat or invitation sent by Elifba. A code passes from person to person outside it. From T2 (ADR 029, amending ADR 024 decision 6): during a running game a player may throw another present player of it a taunt from the closed list of `content/rooms/taunts.json` — an emoji with a short ready phrase — seen for 4 s by its target and the host's screen only — from T3 for 3 s in the middle of the screen, and heard there in the board's prerecorded voice; each device can hide them, the host can switch them off. With the instance's voice on (C3, C4; ADR 031) the players of one room can also talk in it, live, once its host turns the voice on: off in every new room, each player joins and opens their own microphone, a player who arrives after it was turned on waits for the host, the host mutes and removes, and nothing is recorded. Beyond a taunt and the voice there is no contact.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| **No age**                       | Nothing asks one. The area speaks to adults; a younger account holder who joins a friend's room gets the same minimum, the same absence of contact and the same ways out. A room never holds a student or a child: no room route takes `{student}` or `{child}`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| **Nothing computed**             | Beyond a room's ranking: no rating, level or chart of a person, and no claim of a benefit.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| **Deletion**                     | Take your name out of a closed room; delete a room you hosted; delete the account (its rooms go, its rows elsewhere lose the account); `elifba.retention_days`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| **Notice**                       | `/privacy` has a section «Otaqlar» (az + en, built in B7), shown when the switch is on: what is stored, who sees a name and how long, that no age is asked and no player can write to another, how to delete, the operator. From T2 `privacy.rooms.contact` says what a taunt is, what it costs, who sees it (from T3: and hears it, in a prerecorded voice) and how it is hidden or switched off, and `privacy.rooms.stored` names the taunts (ADR 029 decision 30). From ADR 030 one paragraph more (`privacy.rooms.voice`): the screen may say the winner's first name from the built-in name list, the name is sent nowhere and nothing is stored for it. The sections for teachers and parents and the note for a class's parents do not change. With the voice on (C4), the part «Canlı səs» — off until the host turns it on, who hears, never recorded, transport encryption and what the operator could technically hear, the voice server's log — and the voice forms of three sentences and of the welcome page's line (ADR 031 decisions 15–17), which the owner reads before the switch is turned on. |

**Why.** A name among people who share a code, and nothing else, is what a board game around a table gives away. Everything further — an age, a nickname, a chat, a profile — would need data or moderation that Elifba does not have and the owner did not ask for.

**Rejected.** An age or an "I am 18" tick; a parent's consent for a young player; nicknames; first names only; chat (ADR 024). For the voice: recording, an age tick, a nickname or an avatar for a speaker (ADR 031).

### Games first (G2, G3)

**Decision** (G1, 2026-11-18; ADR 026). The owner chose it among three clickable prototypes: the area is known as «Oyunlar», a game is chosen first and the room opens behind that choice, and one start screen — the game, the difficulty, the rounds, the QR and the code, who has joined, «Başla» — stands on the host's phone and on the big screen, driven from either. This subsection is the contract of G2 (the server and the state) and G3 (the pages and the words). Both landed on 2026-11-18 ("As built (G2)" and "As built (G3)" below), so the subsections above read as amended here. Nothing below loosens a rule of a room: every player is still an account, a code still admits and grants nothing else, nothing of a room is shown before joining, nothing is typed, the server alone judges, scores and keeps the clock, and a room page sends and hears no client event.

**What is amended, and where the old text is.**

| Above                                                                                   | From | Reads                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| "The area and the switch": the navigation's one entry, «Otaqlar»                        | G3   | two entries, «Oyunlar» (`/rooms`) and «Keçmiş oyunlar» (`/rooms/past`); the role, its sentence, the group and the switch are as written                                                                                      |
| "Rooms and joining" — Opening: `POST /rooms` → `OpenRoom` (`OpenRoomData`: `plays`)     | G2   | `{mechanic}`: the room opens behind a chosen game, or the open room takes the choice ("Choosing a game" below); `rooms.errors.already_open` has no case left                                                                 |
| "Rooms and joining" — Joining, the code: `/otaq`, `/otaq/{code}`                        | G2   | the entrance is `/oyun`, `/oyun/{code}` under the same route names, limiter and refusals; `GET /otaq` and `GET /otaq/{code}` redirect to them; the QR is `{APP_URL}/oyun/{code}`                                             |
| "Rooms and joining" — the route table's `GET /rooms`: "the code field, «Otaq aç», past" | G2   | `catalog`, `open` and `entrance`; the past rooms are `GET /rooms/past`                                                                                                                                                       |
| "The race" — Starting a game: `StartRoomGame` with `RoomGameSetupData` from the body    | G2   | no body: it starts what the room's choice says (`Room::setup()`); `RoomGameSetupData::rules()` now guards `ChooseRoomGame`                                                                                                   |
| "The race" — The state: `v: 1`                                                          | G2   | `v: 2`, with `setup` ("The state's choice" below); `room.changed` stays `{v: 1, revision}`                                                                                                                                   |
| "The race" — The big screen: "It reads the same state and writes nothing"               | G2   | before a game and after one it sends the host's choice and the start through the host's routes; during a game it writes nothing; it still holds no lock, removal, closing or ending and is still not a credential of its own |
| "Room games" — The pages; "Adults' privacy" — Notice: `/privacy`'s section «Otaqlar»    | G3   | the pages of `docs/frontend.md` rule 15 "Games first"; the section is headed «Böyüklərin oyunları» and gains `privacy.rooms.camera`                                                                                          |

**Words** (G3; ADR 026 decisions 1–5 hold the table of every key). Code names stay — the `Rooms` domain, the tables, `{room}`, `{member}`, the `rooms.*` routes and translation keys. In the interface «oyun» is what a person does (the area, choosing, the code, joining, coming back, leaving, the past list) and «otaq» is written only where a text must name the thing that holds the players and several games: the host's tools («Otağın idarəsi», «Otağı bağla»), the standing across games («Otağın sıralaması»), a closed room, the player role's sentence — unchanged, word for word — and `/privacy`. «Oyunu bitir» ends the game that is running; «Otağı bağla» ends everything and kills the code. The host is «aparıcı».

**Choosing a game** (G2). `POST /rooms` `{mechanic}` (`OpenRoomData::rules()`: one of the mechanics `RoomPlayable` lists that have levels) → `OpenRoom`, under the lock on the host's `users` row:

| The account                         | `OpenRoom`                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| hosts no open room                  | makes the room with its code and the host's player row, and writes the choice: this mechanic at `RoomDefaults`' difficulty and rounds          |
| hosts an open room, no game running | makes no second room: that room's choice takes this mechanic and keeps its difficulty and rounds; the revision rises and the room is signalled |
| hosts an open room, a game running  | writes nothing; the redirect carries `rooms.errors.game_running`                                                                               |

Each redirects to the room. The host is always the room's first player; «Mən də oynayıram» is a tool of the start screen (`SetHostPlays` below).

**The choice** (G2) is three nullable columns of the room row — `rooms.setup_mechanic`, `rooms.setup_difficulty`, `rooms.setup_rounds` (migration `add_setup_to_rooms`; §1 "Tables" gains them with the step) — read through `Room::setup()` as a `RoomGameSetupData`: the stored one, else (a room opened before G2) the latest game's, else the first playable mechanic at the defaults. It is a fact the host wrote: no text, nothing derived, nothing about a person. Only these write it:

- `OpenRoom`, above;
- `Rooms\Actions\ChooseRoomGame` — `PUT /rooms/{room}/setup` with the whole choice `{mechanic, difficulty, rounds}` (`RoomGameSetupData::rules()`, as it guarded the start: only a level `RoomPlayable` can play). Under the room row's lock it refuses a closed room and a room whose game is running, writes the columns, touches `last_activity_at`, raises the revision and signals (`RoomSignal`), and answers 204.

`StartRoomGame` reads it and a game's end leaves it, so **«Yenidən oyna» is `POST /rooms/{room}/games` again** and «Başqa oyun» is `ChooseRoomGame` with another mechanic. `Rooms\Support\RoomDefaults::for($host, $mechanic)` gives a new room its difficulty and rounds: those of the newest game in the newest room the account hosted, when the mechanic has a level at that difficulty and the rounds are inside `elifba.rooms.rounds`; else `elifba.rooms.defaults.difficulty` (2) and `.rounds` (10). It is derived from the account's own rooms at each opening and stored nowhere.

**The state's choice** (G2). `RoomStateData` gains `setup` and `VERSION` becomes 2:

```
RoomState { v: 2, …,                                                       // every other key as in "The state"
  setup: { mechanic, difficulty, rounds, played: bool } | null }           // null: a closed room
```

`Rooms\Data\RoomSetupData`; `RoomSetup`, a strict object, in `resources/js/rooms/contract.ts`. `played` is derived by `RoomState` alone: the room's latest game is over and was played with exactly this mechanic, difficulty and number of rounds. A page shows the final while `played` and the start screen otherwise, and compares nothing itself. The host and every present player get it with the rest of the state — after joining; the join page still shows nothing of the room. Fixtures: each `state.*.json` of an open room gains `setup` (`state.over.json`: `played` true); `state.chosen.json` is new (a game over, another chosen). A page loaded before the deploy that meets a `v` it does not know reloads itself once.

**Who changes it and starts** (G2): the host alone, from any page of the host's session. `rooms.setup.update`, `rooms.games.store` and `rooms.plays.update` are guarded by `RoomPolicy::start` (the host of an open room with no game running) and by nothing about the page; `rooms/screen` sends the first two as `rooms/show` does and gets the same `catalog` (`RoomGameSetupData::options()`; the page prop `setup` of `rooms/show` is renamed to it). `Rooms\Actions\SetHostPlays` — `PUT /rooms/{room}/plays` `{plays}`: under the room row's lock `false` sets the host's own row to left, as `LeaveRoom` does (with `RoomRoundClose` having nothing to close, since no game runs), `true` clears it or makes the row; the lock of «Kilidlə» never stops the host, a full room does (`rooms.errors.full`). It raises the revision, signals and answers 204.

Routes (the player group; a route not listed is as "Rooms and joining" lists it):

| Method and path                                      | Reaches it (policy)                        | Answer                                                             |
| ---------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /rooms`                                         | any player account                         | page `rooms/index`: `catalog`, `open`, `entrance`                  |
| `POST /rooms`                                        | any player account, `throttle:rooms-open`  | `{mechanic}` → `OpenRoom`; redirect to the room                    |
| `GET /rooms/past`                                    | any player account                         | page `rooms/past`: the account's past rooms (`RoomList`)           |
| `GET /oyun`, `GET /oyun/{code}`, `POST /oyun/{code}` | any player account; the entrance's limiter | what `/otaq` answered: `rooms.entrance`, `rooms.join`, `.store`    |
| `GET /otaq`, `GET /otaq/{code}`                      | any player account                         | 301 to `/oyun`, `/oyun/{code}` (`rooms.entrance.old`, `.join.old`) |
| `GET /rooms/{room}`, `GET /rooms/{room}/screen`      | `view`; `screen`                           | as before, and `catalog` for the host of an open room              |
| `PUT /rooms/{room}/setup`                            | `start`                                    | `ChooseRoomGame`; 204                                              |
| `POST /rooms/{room}/games`                           | `start`                                    | `StartRoomGame` with the room's choice, no body; 204               |
| `PUT /rooms/{room}/plays`                            | `start`                                    | `SetHostPlays`; 204                                                |

**The camera** (G3; a page's tool, nothing on the server). The code field has a camera button where the browser can open a camera. The picture is decoded on the device — `BarcodeDetector`, else a decoder bundled with the app under a permissive license and loaded from the app's host — and no frame, picture or decoded text is sent, stored or logged. The scanner accepts only an address of this app whose path is `/oyun/{six digits}` or `/otaq/{six digits}`, never opens what it read, and visits `rooms.join` with the six digits, so a scanned code meets what a typed one meets (`scannedCode()`, Vitest). `/privacy` says it (`privacy.rooms.camera`).

**Why.** People come to play a game, so the game is what they are offered and the room is how it is carried. A choice every device can read needs to be a fact on the server: a row's columns under the room's lock, announced by the signal the pages already hear, cost no new channel, credential or client event. Keeping «otaq» for the thing itself keeps two actions apart that a single word would merge.

**Rejected.** A page per game before the room, or one hub with «yarış» (the other two prototypes); a «Hazır oyun»; no name for the room; `/otaq` as the shown address, or dropping it; the choice as a client event or in the broadcast; a second room for a host who has one; the last choice stored on the account; the choice in the start's body; a ready button; scanning through a third party or an upload; the children's catalog's components (ADR 026).

**As built (G2, 2026-11-18).** The server and the state are as above; where the build says more, or other, it is this:

- **Opening while a game runs.** `OpenRoom` throws `Rooms\Exceptions\RoomGameRunning` (with the room) and writes nothing; `RoomController::store` answers with the redirect to the room and an error toast (`Inertia::flash('toast', …)`) saying `rooms.errors.game_running`. The `rooms-open` limiter (6 a minute) counts a game chosen for the open room too. `rooms.errors.already_open` is gone from both languages.
- **The open room taking a game** keeps its difficulty and rounds only while the game has a level at that difficulty (`RoomGameSetupData::withMechanic()`); otherwise it takes `RoomDefaults` for that game, as a new room would.
- **`RoomDefaults`** reads the newest game of the rooms the account hosted — the newest room that has one, its last game — not only the newest room, so a room opened and left without a game does not reset the defaults; a game the account played in someone else's room never counts. When the chosen game has no level at the default difficulty it takes the game's lowest.
- **`Room::setup()`** reads a stored choice only while it is still playable (`RoomGameSetupData::isPlayable()`: a level `RoomPlayable` plays, rounds inside the bounds), since content and config may change under an open room; else the latest game's, else `RoomDefaults` for the stored game or the first one.
- **`ChooseRoomGame`** changes nothing and signals nothing when the room already has that choice, as `LockRoom` does for the lock. **`SetHostPlays`** refuses a closed room and a running game under the lock as well (403), changes nothing when the host already is where asked, and marks the room active when it changes.
- **The state.** `RoomStateData` holds `setup` null exactly when the room is closed (its constructor refuses the rest), and `fromArray()` refuses another `v`. On the page side `ROOM_STATE_VERSION` is 2, and `fetchState` (`resources/js/rooms/http.ts`) answers `refused` for a state whose `v` is another number, so the page reloads — through Inertia, whose asset version brings the new pages after a deploy — rather than ask again and again.
- **The lists.** `RoomList::open()` (each open room with `mechanic`, its choice's, and `running`) and `RoomList::past()` replace `RoomList::of()`; `RoomJoinLink::entrance()` is the code field's address `rooms/index` gets as `entrance`.
- **Pages, plain until G3** (G3 replaced them: "As built (G3)" below). `rooms/index` lists the games by family as buttons that post `{mechanic}`, the open rooms with their game, the code field and a link to `rooms/past`, which lists the past rooms as `rooms/index` did. `rooms/show`'s dialog (`components/rooms/game-setup.tsx`) shows the state's `setup`, sends each tap as the whole choice to `rooms.setup.update` (its failure is `rooms.setup.change_failed`, the one other key G2 adds) and starts with no body. `rooms/screen` gets `catalog` and draws nothing of it yet; nothing calls `rooms.plays.update` yet. Neither the navigation nor any text changed besides the keys named here.
- **Tests' helpers.** `Tests\Support\Rooms::open($host, $plays, $mechanic)` opens through `OpenRoom` and steps the host out through `SetHostPlays`; `playing()` chooses through `ChooseRoomGame` and starts through `StartRoomGame`, so a started game is one revision later than before G2.

**As built (G3, 2026-11-18).** The pages are docs/frontend.md rule 15 "Games first", with its "As built (G3)"; the words are ADR 026 decision 4's in both languages. What G3 did on the server, or beyond this text:

- **Two props a page lacked**, and nothing else on the server: `rooms/past` gets `retention_days` (`elifba.retention_days`, for its line «… :days gün saxlanılır»), and `rooms/join` gets `entrance` at `/oyun` (`RoomJoinLink::entrance()`; null at `/oyun/{code}`), whose origin its camera accepts as `rooms/index`'s does. `RoomPagesTest` and `JoinRoomTest` assert both.
- **The camera** decodes with the browser's own `BarcodeDetector` where it reads `qr_code` (Chrome, Edge, Android), else with `jsqr` 1.4.0 (Apache-2.0, added with the owner's OK the same day), imported only when the scanner opens, so it is its own chunk (`jsQR-*.js`) served from the app's host: each frame is drawn at most 640px on a canvas that never leaves the page and read there. `canScan()` asks only for a camera (`getUserMedia` in a secure context), so the button shows on Safari too; a decoder that could not be had is said as the refused camera is. `scannedCode()` and `scanOrigins()` are `resources/js/rooms/scan.ts` with their Vitest cases; the jsQR path was seen in headless Chrome with `BarcodeDetector` taken away (a foreign QR refused, the room's QR joining, the chunk from the app's host, no other request).
- **Three keys beyond decision 4's table:** `rooms.show.results` («Nəticələr» / "Results"), a closed room's title now that `rooms.show.title` is gone; `rooms.start.leader` (`:place: :name · :points`), the first place on the standing card; `rooms.tools.lock_line` («Kilidləsəniz, yeni oyunçu qoşula bilməz.»), «Kilidlə»'s line while the lock is off. `privacy.rooms.delete` and `.how_long` say «aparıcı» where they said «otağı açan» too, as decision 3 has it; their meaning is unchanged. Four keys the new pages no longer say are gone: `rooms.show.away`, `.players_count`, `.unlock` (a switch now) and `rooms.screen.next_join`; `rooms.show.host` («aparıcı») marks the host's chip on the screen.
- **The player's navigation** is three areas — «Oyunlar» (`/rooms`), «Keçmiş oyunlar» (`/rooms/past`), Ayarlar — and `activeArea()` (`lib/nav-active.ts`) takes the area with the longest `base` that holds the page, so `/rooms/past` is its own entry though it lies under `/rooms`.
- **The READMEs' pictures:** `docs/screenshots/room-start-screen.png` (1280 × 720) and `room-start-phone.png` (750 × 1334) are new — the start screen on the screen and on the host's phone, with `elifba.io/oyun` — and `room-screen.png` and `room-phone.png` were taken again (round 3 of 5 of «Düzgün yazılış»), all with made-up names. The welcome page's two pictures (`resources/images/welcome/rooms-*.webp`) show a round and were left as they were.
- **Tests it adds:** `RoomsWordingTest` "says «otaq» only where the thing itself is named" (the keys decision 2 allows, read in `lang/az.json`) and the READMEs' sections under «Böyüklər üçün oyunlar» / "Games for adults"; `PrivacyPageTest` "says what the camera does with the picture"; `scan.test.ts`; `nav-active.test.ts` "finds the past games as their own entry, though under /rooms"; `rooms-switch.test.ts` passes over a short label the welcome page's other sections also say («Oyun seç» in «Oyun seçimi…»). Seen in a browser (headless Chrome over CDP on a scratch Postgres 17 and Reverb, three players and the screen; light, dark and reduced motion): the scripts and shots are outside the repository.

**Guard tests** (beside the table above, which holds):

| Test                                                                         | What it gains                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `tests/Feature/Rooms/ChooseGameTest.php` (new, G2)                           | "opens a room behind the chosen game", "gives the choice to the open room instead of opening a second", "changes nothing while a game is running", "lets only the host change the choice or start", "shows the choice to the host and a present player, and nothing of it before joining", "starts what the room's choice says" |
| `tests/Feature/Classroom/TeacherIsolationTest.php`                           | G2: a case for `rooms.setup.update`, `rooms.plays.update`, `rooms.past` and the `/oyun` routes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `tests/Feature/Auth/RoleAreasTest.php`, `RoomsSwitchTest`, `BearerReachTest` | G2: the new routes are in the player group, 404 while the switch is off, and answer a guest board's bearer as they answer a visitor                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `tests/Feature/Rooms/RoomsApartTest.php`                                     | G3: still "keeps the room pages apart from the children's on the page side" — the grid of games imports nothing of `components/games`                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `tests/Unit/RoomsWordingTest.php`                                            | G3: reads the new keys and the READMEs' section under its new heading                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `resources/js/rooms/scan.test.ts` (new, G3)                                  | "takes the code from this app's own join address", "takes the old /otaq address too", "refuses another site's address, another path and a bare number"                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Rivals face to face (P2)

**Decision** (P1, 2026-11-18; ADR 027). The owner chose it among three clickable prototypes — variant A, «Zolaq», in blue and orange, then its finished look with three Fluent emoji: a live room game shows its players as rivals in one PK strip at the top, on every phone and on the screen, all game long, and it moves only in a reveal. This subsection is the contract of P2, which landed on 2026-11-18 ("As built (P2)" below). Nothing below loosens a rule of a room: the server alone judges, scores and keeps the clock, the state and its version do not change, nothing tells right from wrong before the reveal, and nothing about a person is added.

**What is amended, and where the old text is.**

| Above, or elsewhere                                                  | Reads                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| "The race" — The big screen: "the reveal and the ranking"            | with three players or more the reveal's ranking is the race lanes (below); with two, the plain `Standing`; the strip stands above the round in every phase                               |
| "Scoring and what is kept" — Who sees it                             | also as the strip: the pair a device shows is two rows of the same ranking, to the same people                                                                                           |
| ADR 025 decision 5: "Nothing ranks, adds up or judges on the device" | a page may take rows of the ranking in the server's order and show the difference of two of its totals and their ratio as a bar; it never orders, sums or judges                         |
| ADR 025 decisions 6 and 10                                           | an overtake's words stand still under reduced motion where the page drew the pair before; the screen's reveal with three players or more draws `RaceLanes`, every other plain list stays |

**The pair** (`rivalPair(state, viewer)`, `resources/js/components/rooms/rivalry.ts`, pure, Vitest), from the state as it is (`v: 2`; `RoomStateData`, `contract.ts` and the fixtures unchanged):

```
contenders = ranking rows, in the server's order, whose player holds a place or is present
             (players[].present; a player who left keeps a place while their points stand;
              one who left without points, or a host who does not play, is not one)
< 2        → no strip
= 2 (duel) → a player's phone: [viewer, the other]
             the screen, or a host who does not play: the two in the order of players (as they joined)
≥ 3 (race) → a player's phone: [viewer, contenders[0] ≠ viewer ? contenders[0] : contenders[1]]
             the screen, or a host who does not play: [contenders[0], contenders[1]]
             a right side (on the screen, either side) only while its row holds a place
gap   = left.points − right.points          // sign: who leads; |gap|: the number shown
split = clamp(left ÷ (left + right), 12 %, 88 %), 50 % at 0 : 0   // for show
more  = the other contenders sharing the right side's place     // «+:count nəfər», «və daha :count nəfər»
lead  = the side with more points; none when level
overtakeOf(previous, current) = current.lead ≠ previous.lead, current.lead not none, previous had two sides
```

`previous` is the pair the page last drew — in the round's question, or in the reveal before; a page with none shows no overtake. The mode follows the contenders, so a third player joining during a game turns a duel into a race.

**The strip** (`RivalryStrip`, `components/rooms/rivalry-strip.tsx`; `big` on the screen) stands above `RoundHead` in `LiveRound` and, on the screen, under the top bar above the round's line, in the countdown, the study, the question and the reveal of a live game — never in the start screen, the final or a closed room. It is a card with the sides' tints: a names row (each side's place as a filled label in its colour from `place_labels`, not in a duel; the name as the state gives it or «Siz»; 👑 by a side holding place 1; «çıxıb»; the shared-place count), the bar (each side's gradient, the totals inside its ends, ⚡ on the meeting point) and the gap's pill (an arrow disc in the leading side's colour and the words); 87px on the phone, the bar 6.4vh on the screen. The sides are sky (left) and orange (right) in both themes, never emerald, red or the study's amber; on a phone «Siz» is always the left side. Before any row holds a place a race shows a grey bar without a split or ⚡ and «Hələ xal yoxdur»; a duel shows 0 : 0 in its colours. Its `role="group"` has the `aria-label` `rooms.rivalry.label`; the emoji are `alt=""` and `aria-hidden`.

**Motion and the reveal** (ADR 025's rules, ADR 027 decisions 12–18). During the countdown, the study and the question it stands still. In a reveal the page saw begin, at the ranking's moment and 250 ms on, the split slides in 700 ms while both totals count up from `points − result.points`, then the gap's words change; an overtake adds one shine across the bar and a ribbon — 🔥 and «Önə keçdiniz!» for the viewer who took the lead, else «:name önə keçdi!» — in the leading side's colour, on the phone a row of its own inside the card, on the screen over the middle of the bar, until the next round opens. When the pair's members or sides changed, the strip first moves with the sides it showed and their new totals, then the sides take their new places. Under `prefers-reduced-motion` everything stands at its new value, with no shine, and the ribbon stands still where the page drew the pair before; a page opened on a reveal shows no ribbon. No sound is added (the screen's `ranking` cue already marks the moment). The phone's reveal keeps the plain «Sıralama». The screen's reveal with three players or more puts the solution beside `RaceLanes` (`components/rooms/race-lanes.tsx`): a row per ranking row in the server's order — the place label once per place, the name with 👑 at place 1 and «çıxıb», a bar of the row's points against the first row's, the round's result, the total; the strip's two rows tinted; one column up to ten rows, two from eleven; rows moving by `rankShift()`. The final is `FinalStanding`, unchanged.

**The emoji** are content. Three Fluent Emoji (Color) files of commit `1ffb34c`, unmodified — `26A1.svg` (⚡), `1F451.svg` (👑), `1F525.svg` (🔥) — enter `content/assets/fluent/` under its existing credit; `content/rooms/rivalry.json` names them (table "Room content"); `ContentRepository::roomRivalry()` reads it into `Content\Data\RoomRivalryData`, whose `urls()` are `ContentAssets::url()` addresses; `rooms/show` gets them as the prop `rivalry` (`{clash, leader, overtake}`) while it follows an open room, `rooms/screen` always, and every other page nothing. They show the state — a meeting, the first place, a change of lead — and never stand for a person.

**Fit.** The question's grid limit goes from `100svh − 22rem` to about `100svh − 26.5rem` (as built `100svh − 28.5rem`, below): at 375 × 667 with the shell, a 4 × 4 grid keeps cells of at least 44px (56px in the prototype) and the 8-letter anagram fits with the row under it, nothing scrolling during a question. From F2 the page has no shell and the limit is `100svh − 21.25rem` ("The game frame (F2)").

**Words** (`rooms.rivalry.*`, ADR 027 decision 19): `label`, `you`, `ahead`, `behind`, `level`, `leads`, `level_others`, `no_points`, `took_lead_you`, `took_lead`, `more_short`, `more`, `away`.

**Why.** The state already carries the ranking in every phase, and the order is the server's: two of its rows and the difference of their totals need no new field, no version and no reload. A ranking that counts only closed rounds cannot move while a round is open, so a strip drawn from it is fair by construction.

**Rejected.** Variants B («Üz-üzə») and C («Arena»); a `rivals` field in the state (`v: 3`); the player just above as the rival; the leader always on the left of a phone; blue and fuchsia, emerald and red, a colour per player; Unicode emoji from the device's font, more emoji; idle movement; an overtake without a pair the page drew; a sound for it; the strip in the final; initials or avatars (ADR 027).

**Guard tests** (P2, beside the tables above):

| Test                                                  | What it gains                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `resources/js/components/rooms/rivalry.test.ts`       | "puts the viewer against the leader, or the runner-up when the viewer leads"; "keeps a duel's sides in the order the players joined on the screen"; "puts the first place left and the second right on the screen"; "keeps a player who left while they hold a place, and drops one without points and a host who does not play"; "has no rival before anyone holds a place"; "shows a shared place as one side and a count"; "never reorders the server's rows"; "finds an overtake only between two pairs the page drew, and none for drawing level" |
| `resources/js/components/rooms/motion.test.ts`        | "draws the strip still during a question and on a reveal the page opened on"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `tests/Feature/Rooms/RoomPagesTest.php`               | "sends the rivalry icons to the room page of an open room and to the screen, and none to a closed room"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `tests/Unit/Content/ContentValidatorTest.php`         | `rivalry.json`: exactly `clash`, `leader`, `overtake`, each an SVG that exists and passes the SVG check, one broken fixture per rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `tests/Unit/RoomsWordingTest.php`, `TranslationsTest` | the `rooms.rivalry.*` keys in both languages, no promise of a benefit                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `tests/Feature/Rooms/RoomsApartTest.php`              | still "keeps the room pages apart from the children's on the page side": the strip and the lanes import nothing of the children's side                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

**As built (P2, 2026-11-18).** The pages, the words and the content as above, with ADR 027's "As built (P2)"; what the build adds or had to change:

- **Code.** `components/rooms/rivalry.ts` (`rivalPair()`, `overtakeOf()`, `contenders()`, `splitOf()`, `interimPair()` — the sides drawn before with their new totals — and `pairBefore()` — the pair at `points − result.points`), `rivalry-strip.tsx` (`RivalryStrip`; `useRivalry(state, game, round, viewer)`, which keeps the pair the page drew before; the screen passes the viewer null, since it is the host's session), `race-lanes.tsx` (`RaceLanes`), `screen-fit.tsx` (`ScreenFit`), `motion.ts` (`stripMoves()`, carried as `RoomMotion.strip`), `motion-dom.ts` (`slideSlots()`, rows moving slot to slot in one column or two; every movement's distance divided by the element's CSS zoom). `Standing`'s `RoundResult` is exported for the lanes. `app.css` adds `--animate-room-shine` and `--animate-room-drop`, and the sides' tokens `--rival-{left,right}{,-hi,-text,-tint}` (Tailwind `rival-*`).
- **The phone's grid limit is `100svh − 28.5rem`** (from F2 `100svh − 21.25rem`, every grid fitting 375 × 667: "The game frame (F2)"). The strip with the section's gap is 103px: with 26.5rem a 4 × 4 question was 31px taller than 667px. With 28.5rem it fits, its cells about 47px. The 5 × 5 and 6 × 6 grids (difficulties 3 and 4) keep 44px cells and scroll at 375 × 667 by about 32px and 82px (they fit from about 700px and 750px of height); the 8-letter anagram fits. The host's phone may scroll to «Oyunu bitir» under the round, as it could before.
- **The screen fits its round under the strip.** With the strip the mechanics' `vh` sizes no longer fitted 1280 × 720 (a 4 × 4 grid is 62vh; spelling and the countdown overflowed too). While a round is shown the screen is one screen high (`h-dvh`), and `ScreenFit` draws the round's body smaller — never larger — by CSS `zoom` where it does not fit: measured when a moment is drawn and when the window changes size, never because of a player; a question never larger than its study, so the grid keeps the study's place; in the reveal the solution and the ranking each in its column. Every mechanic at difficulty 4 fits so.
- **A race with one place held.** Only a row holding a place stands on the right (on the screen on either side), so a single scorer stands alone on the left: the grey bar of "Before anybody scores" with their total, no split, no ⚡, but the gap said in full — `gap` is their points, since every other contender has 0 («15 xal öndəsiniz», «:name 15 xal öndədir»). «Hələ xal yoxdur» is for no place held.
- **Motion.** The split slides and the totals count from `REVEAL_MS.ranking` + 250 ms for 700 ms; then the gap's words pop (the pill shows the words drawn before until then) and an overtake's light crosses the bar; 80 ms later changed sides drop into place and the ribbon pops. Under reduced motion it is all drawn at once and the ribbon stands still; a page opened on the reveal shows the strip without a ribbon (seen in the browser).
- **Content.** `ContentValidator` also requires each icon under `assets/fluent/` and no file for two icons; the fixture root has `rooms/rivalry.json` and three plain `assets/fluent/*.svg` under a credit of their own.
- **Tests** as the table above, with these names: `rivalry.test.ts` adds "splits the bar by the two totals, never letting a side vanish", "moves the sides the strip showed first, with their new totals, when the pair's members or sides changed" and "starts from the totals before the round"; `motion.test.ts`'s case is on `stripMoves()`; `RoomsWordingTest` adds "words the rivalry strip in exactly the strings ADR 027 lists, in both languages".

### Taunts (T2)

**Decision** (T1, 2026-11-18; ADR 029). During a running game a player may throw a rival a ready-made taunt — an emoji with a short phrase from our own list of ten — and it costs the thrower one point; the target loses nothing. Of three clickable prototypes, variant A, «Zolaqda», was taken under the owner's standing rule of 2026-11-18 (a session takes its recommended option; the owner may reverse it): the tray stands beside the strip of "Rivals face to face (P2)" (ADR 027) and throws at the strip's rival; a received taunt lies over the strip on the target's phone for 4 s, and the screen shows it over its own strip. This subsection is the contract of T2, which builds on P2's strip. Nothing below loosens a rule of a room: nothing is typed, the server alone scores and keeps the clock, the channel carries a revision only, and nothing of the open round is told before its close.

**What is amended, and where the old text is.**

| Above                                                                                                       | From | Reads                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| "Scoring and what is kept": "nothing is negative"; `ofGame()` sums the answers' points                      | T2   | an answer's points are unchanged and never negative; a game's are its answers' less its counted taunts, never below 0 (ADR 022 as amended)                                      |
| "The race" — An answer: "Each player's device writes that player's own answer"                              | T2   | it also writes that player's own taunts, through `ThrowRoomTaunt` (ADR 021 decision 1 as amended)                                                                               |
| "The race" — The state: `v: 1` (`v: 2` from G2)                                                             | T2   | `v: 3`, with `room.taunts`, `taunts` and `round.reveal.spent` (below); `room.changed` stays `{v: 1, revision}`                                                                  |
| "The race" — The channel: the facts that raise the revision                                                 | T2   | also an accepted taunt and the host's switch                                                                                                                                    |
| "Adults' privacy": "Minimum data", "Nothing typed", "No contact", "Notice"                                  | T2   | as that table now reads (ADR 024 decisions 2, 6 and 10 as amended)                                                                                                              |
| "Rivals face to face (P2)" (ADR 027 decisions 1, 11, 13, 14; on `feat/rooms-rivalry` when this was written) | T2   | the strip also reads `round.reveal.spent`, totals and the split move from `points − result.points + spent`, the lanes show the cost; on a phone the tray shares the strip's row |

**The cost** (ADR 029 decisions 1–7).

- `RoomPointsRule::TAUNT_COST` is 1 — a constant of the one points rule, PHP only, never config, so a change never re-prices a game played.
- A taunt **counts from the first close after it**: thrown in round i's countdown, study or answer, from `closes(i)`; in round i's reveal, from `closes(i + 1)`; every taunt of a game once the game is over (its last close, `ended_ms`, the room's close). `RoomRanking::ofGame()` and `::ofRoom()` subtract `TAUNT_COST` for each counted taunt of a player, so the ranking — and the strip — still changes only at a close.
- **The balance** — what a player may spend now — is the answers' points over the game's closed rounds less every taunt the player threw in the game, counted or not (`Rooms\Support\RoomTauntBalance`, derived, never stored). A throw needs a balance of at least 1, read under the room row's lock, so two taps or two devices never spend one point twice; no balance or total goes below 0. Only the running game's points can be spent.
- **Nobody can throw before the first round closes**: every balance is 0 until then.
- **Nothing leaks**: the balance reads closed rounds and the player's own taunts only; the open round's answers never enter it, so the −1 a thrower sees at once is the same whatever anybody answered (ADR 025 decisions 3 and 14).
- **Where it shows**: on the thrower's own phone at once (the tray's balance, «Atıldı · −1 xal»); everywhere else at the next reveal — «−:count» with the taunt icon beside the round's result in «Sıralama» and the race lanes, «Atmaca: −:count xal» in the thrower's verdict — with totals counting up from `points − result.points + spent`.
- **After the game the net only**: the final, a closed room, `/rooms/past` show net points; who taunted whom, or how often, is shown nowhere.

**Who, when, how often** (decisions 8–12).

- The **thrower** is a present player of an open room with a live game; a host who does not play, a player who left and a removed one throw nothing. The **target** is any present player of the same room other than the thrower — the server's rule; variant A's phone offers only the strip's rival (`rivalPair()`), and the server does not mirror that device rule.
- The **window** is from the game's start until its last round closes: the countdown, the study, the answer and every reveal but the last. Never the lobby, the start screen, the last reveal, the final or a closed room.
- **Once per `elifba.rooms.taunts.cooldown_ms`** (10 000) per thrower, by `RoomClock`, under the lock; the limiter `room-taunts`, 20 a minute per account. Every accepted taunt makes every device read the state once more; the cooldown bounds that.
- **The host's switch** — `rooms.taunts`, on by default — «Atmacalar» in «Otağın idarəsi» and on the start screen: `PUT /rooms/{room}/taunts` `{on}` → `Rooms\Actions\SetRoomTaunts`, the host of an open room at any time, under the room row's lock, raising the revision and signalling; 204. Off, the tray goes, the server refuses and no taunt reaches a device; costs paid stay.

**The write** (decision 13). `POST /rooms/{room}/taunts` `{event_id, target_id, taunt}` (`Rooms\Data\RoomTauntData::rules()`: a ULID, a ULID, a key of the list) → `Rooms\Actions\ThrowRoomTaunt`, in one transaction under the room row's lock:

1. the `event_id` is stored → `duplicate`;
2. no live game, its last round closed, a closed room, or the thrower not present → `late`, nothing written;
3. the host's switch is off → `off`;
4. `target_id` is not a present player of the room, or is the thrower → 422, nothing written;
5. the thrower's latest taunt of the game is younger than the cooldown → `cooling`;
6. the balance is below 1 → `no_points`;
7. otherwise stored with `sent_ms` = `RoomClock::nowMs()`, `last_activity_at` touched, the revision raised, `RoomChanged` after commit → `accepted`.

The answer is `{status, revision}` (`Rooms\Data\RoomTauntReceiptData`, JSON, `no-store`). A taunt never closes a round, never counts as an answer and never as a read: no `RoomRoundClose`, no `seen_ms`, nothing `RoomWaits` reads.

**The state** (decision 15):

```
RoomState { v: 3, …,                                         // every other key as in v 2
  room:   { …, taunts: bool },                               // the host's switch
  taunts: RoomTaunts | null,                                 // null: no live game, or a closed room
  round:  { …, reveal: { …, spent: [{ player_id, count }] } } // the taunts counted at this close; only in the reveal
}
RoomTaunts {
  open: bool,                                                // a throw is accepted now but for the balance and the cooldown
  balance: int | null,                                       // the viewer's; null: the viewer does not play
  ready_ms: moment | null,                                   // the end of the viewer's cooldown, while it runs
  received: [{ id, from, taunt, sent_ms }],                  // at the viewer, within show_ms of server_ms, newest first, ≤ 3
  shown: [{ id, from, to, taunt, sent_ms }] | null           // GET …/state?for=screen only (RoomPolicy::screen); null otherwise
}
```

`from` and `to` are `room_players.id`. A taunt is in a state only within `elifba.rooms.taunts.show_ms` (4 000) of its throw and only for its target and the screen; the thrower's own taunts are not listed; `received` is `[]` while the switch is off; nothing after the game. PHP `Rooms\Data\{RoomTauntsData,RoomTauntSeenData,RoomSpentData}`, zod `RoomTaunts` (strict); every `state.*.json` of an open room gains `room.taunts` and `taunts`, `state.reveal.json` gains `spent`, and `state.taunted.json`, `state.screen-taunted.json`, `taunt.json` and `taunt-receipt.json` are new. A page meeting `v: 3` before the deploy reloads once (G2).

**Config** (`config/elifba.php`, `rooms.taunts`): `cooldown_ms` 10 000, `show_ms` 4 000, `forget_target_hours` 24.

Routes (the player group):

| Method and path                      | Reaches it (policy)            | Answer                                                    |
| ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `POST /rooms/{room}/taunts`          | `play`, `throttle:room-taunts` | `ThrowRoomTaunt`; `{status, revision}` (JSON, `no-store`) |
| `PUT /rooms/{room}/taunts`           | `host`, an open room           | `SetRoomTaunts`; 204                                      |
| `GET /rooms/{room}/state?for=screen` | `screen`                       | the state with `taunts.shown`; without `for`, as before   |

**The list** (decisions 27–28; replaced by T3's twelve, below). Ten taunts, about the game only — slowness, sleepiness, luck, bragging: 🐢 «Bir az tez ol!», ⏰ «Tik-tak, tik-tak!», 🥶 «Donub qaldın?», 😴 «Yatma, oyun gedir!», 🍀 «Sadəcə bəxtin gətirdi!», 🎲 «Zərlə seçirsən?», 😎 «Bu raund mənimdir!», 🚀 «Yetiş görüm!», 🥱 «Çox asan idi!», 😅 «Az qaldı, hə?» (keys, files and English in ADR 029 decision 27). `content/rooms/taunts.json` — a row of "Room content" from T2: `version` and `taunts` (`{key, image}`, each image a Fluent Emoji file of `content/assets/fluent/`, as `rivalry.json` names its three); `ContentRepository::roomTaunts()` → `Content\Data\RoomTauntData`; `ContentValidator` requires 8–12 taunts, unique keys of `^[a-z_]{2,32}$`, each image an existing SVG of that folder that passes the SVG check, none named twice and none of the strip's three. The phrases are `rooms.taunts.say.{key}` in both languages. `rooms/show` and `rooms/screen` get `taunts` (`[{key, url}]`) while they follow an open room, null otherwise; each device draws a phrase in its own locale.

**The pages** are docs/frontend.md rule 15 "Taunts": the tray beside the strip, the popover with the ten, «Atıldı · −1 xal», the card over the target's strip, the screen's cards and its «Atmacalar» toggle, «Mənə atılanları gizlət» (`localStorage`), the reveal's «−:count»; violet, never right's, wrong's, a side's or the lamp's colour; no sound. T3 replaces the tray, the card and the silence: "Taunts in the middle, said aloud (T3)" below.

**Privacy** (decisions 29–32). A `room_taunts` row: the game, the thrower, the target, the key, the moment. `PruneOldRooms` (daily) empties `target_player_id` once a taunt is older than `forget_target_hours`; the rest goes with the room. `ForgetRoomPlayer` and `RemoveRoomPlayer` leave a player's taunts, so no place moves. `/privacy` changes `privacy.rooms.contact` and gains a line in `privacy.rooms.stored` (both languages, ADR 029 decision 30); the player role's sentence does not change.

**Guard tests** (T2; the table above holds):

| Test                                                                          | What it gains                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `tests/Feature/Rooms/RoomTauntsTest.php` (new)                                | "takes one point from the thrower and none from the target"; "counts a taunt from the first close after it, and every one once the game is over"; "refuses a throw with no points, and two at once never spend one point twice"; "stores a taunt once by its event_id"; "refuses in the lobby, the first round's countdown, the last reveal, the final, a closed room and while switched off"; "refuses oneself, a player who left, a removed player and a host who does not play"; "keeps the cooldown by the server's clock"; "never closes a round, counts as an answer or as a read"; "shows a taunt only to its target and the screen, and only for show_ms"; "shows who taunted whom nowhere after the game"; "keeps the cost when a player takes their name out or is removed"; "forgets the target after a day"; "holds exactly the approved list"; "names a phrase for every taunt in both languages and no other" |
| `RoomContractTest`, `resources/js/rooms/contract.test.ts`                     | the `v: 3` fixtures, strict both ways                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ContentValidatorTest`                                                        | one broken fixture per rule of `taunts.json`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `RoleAreasTest`, `RoomsSwitchTest`, `BearerReachTest`, `TeacherIsolationTest` | `rooms.taunts.store` and `rooms.taunts.update` in the player group, a 404 while the switch is off, a guest board's bearer answered as a visitor, an account outside the room 404 and changing nothing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `RoomPagesTest`, `PrivacyPageTest`                                            | "sends the room page and the screen the taunts' images, and a closed room none"; "says what a taunt is, what it costs, who sees it and how it is hidden"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `resources/js/components/rooms/taunts.test.ts` (new)                          | "shows a taunt the page heard arrive once, however often the state comes again"; "draws a taunt the page opened on still"; "drops a taunt at sent_ms + show_ms by the server's clock"; "shows nothing while this device hides taunts"; "never reads a verdict, a solution or anyone's result before the reveal"                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `RoomsApartTest`                                                              | no room file imports the children's catalog for the taunts' images                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

**Why.** A taunt the thrower pays for keeps teasing a choice, and a list of our own keeps it a joke anyone may read: nothing typed, nothing to moderate. Counting the cost at a close keeps the one rule of the race — nothing moves before the reveal — and a balance blind to the open round can never be read as a verdict. The strip is where the rivalry already is, so the tray and the card take no height from the round.

**Rejected.** A head button with a sheet and every present player, a toast over the header and a silent screen (B, «Panel»); a row of emoji always under the round and the screen as a stage (C, «Meydan»: the 4 × 4 cells fell to 43px); the −1 at once in the strip and the ranking; a stored balance; a taunt that takes the target's point; spending an earlier game's standing; taunts in the lobby, the last reveal or the final; a taunt at everyone; typed text or the system's emoji; sound (reversed by T3, below); a client event or anything on the channel; storing the phrase; keeping who taunted whom or a count of taunts after the game; a per-target cap (ADR 029).

**As built (T2, 2026-11-18).** The server and the state are as above; where the build says more, or other, it is this:

- **Two support classes** hold the rules the text gives: `Rooms\Support\RoomTauntCost` — `countsAt()`, the first close of the game strictly after the throw (a close's own moment is already its reveal, so a taunt thrown at that moment counts at the next close), `counted()`, `spent()` for the ranking and `spentAt()` for a reveal's `spent` — and `Rooms\Support\RoomTauntWindow::open()`, an open room whose latest game is not over and has not passed its last close. `RoomRanking::ofRoom()` nets each game on its own (`max(0, answers − cost)`) and adds the games up, with one query for the answers and one for the taunts of all the room's games.
- **`ThrowRoomTaunt`** answers the 422 on `target_id` with the sentence of `rooms.taunts.late`; the target's row must be present in the room (`RoomPlayer::present()`), so a removed player, one who left, a host who does not play and a row of another room are all refused the same way. A thrower who is not a present player of an open room never reaches it (`RoomPolicy::play`, 403).
- **The state.** `RoomTauntsData::MAX_LISTED` (3) bounds `shown` as it bounds `received`, newest first. `RoomTauntSeenData` serializes `to` only in the screen's `shown`; `RoomTauntsData` refuses a received taunt with a target, a shown one without, and a balance below 0, and `RoomStateData` refuses `taunts` on anything but a live game. `GET …/state?for=screen` records no read, like a request from the screen's page; `rooms/screen`'s first props are the screen's state (`RoomState::for(…, screen: true)`).
- **The prop** `taunts` is `Content\Data\RoomTauntData::props()`; a page draws the emoji from its URL only, never from a path of its own (`RoomsApartTest`).
- **Content.** The nine new files were fetched unmodified from the pinned commit, each checked against its folder's `metadata.json`; `1F422.svg` stays shared with the children's animals pack, and rooms read it only through `taunts.json`. `ContentValidator` also refuses a key besides `key` and `image` — a phrase in the file — and a `taunts` that is not a list. The rule "none of the strip's three" reads `rooms/rivalry.json`.
- **The pages** are rule 15 "Taunts" with its "As built (T2)": the tray and the received card beside and over ADR 027's strip (which landed first), the screen's cards and toggle, the host's switch in «Otağın idarəsi» and «Oyun ayarları», and the reveal's cost in `Standing`, the race lanes and the strip's count (`pairBefore(pair, results, spent)`). Seen in a browser, light and dark, az and en, with and without reduced motion, with two players and with seven.
- **Tests' guards.** `RoomRaceTest` and `RoomPagesTest` look for the reveal's keys as keys (`"mine":`), since `mine` is a taunt's key too; `RoomTauntsTest` also holds the page's `TAUNT_SHOW_MS` equal to `elifba.rooms.taunts.show_ms`; `RoomsSwitchTest`, `BearerReachTest` and `TeacherIsolationTest` snapshot `room_taunts` with the other room tables.

### A room without a screen (2026-11-19)

**Decision** (the owner, 2026-11-19, after playing on elifba.io with phones only; ADR 028 "Amended 2026-11-19"). In a room without a big screen every phone plays the room's cues too — the countdown, `go`, the clock, the close and the ranking, the final — beside its own, and (the same day, the owner: «oyunçular əsasən mobildə oynayır», ADR 030 "Amended 2026-11-19") the winner's cheer and the final's confetti for everyone; with a screen nothing changes.

**What is amended, and where the old text is.**

| Above                                                                                                | Reads                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| "Room games": "a phone plays only quiet cues of its own"; "A page gets only its own speaker's files" | with a screen, as written; without one a phone plays the room's cues too, and `rooms/show` gets both sets in `sounds`           |
| "The race" — The state: `v: 3` (T2)                                                                  | `v: 4`, with `room.screen`; `room.changed` stays `{v: 1, revision}`                                                             |
| "The big screen": opening it writes nothing                                                          | opening it and each of its state requests (`?for=screen`) write the screen's own read, `rooms.screen_seen_ms`, and nothing else |

**The screen's read.** While it is shown, the screen reads the state at least every `elifba.rooms.screen.beat_ms` (10 000; the prop `beat_ms`), and a hidden screen asks nothing (`useLiveRoom({ beatMs })`). Opening `rooms/screen` and `GET /rooms/{room}/state?for=screen` — the host of an open room only (`RoomPolicy::screen`) — call `Rooms\Actions\RecordScreenRead`, the only writer of `rooms.screen_seen_ms`: one conditional update at most once a second, without the room's lock, the revision, a signal, `updated_at` or `last_activity_at`. A player's read, the host's phone and a request from the screen's page without `for=screen` record nothing.

**The state** is `v: 4`: `room.screen` is `Rooms\Support\RoomScreen::present()` — an open room whose screen read within `goneMs()`, three beats. Derived, never stored; a phone learns of a screen that came or went at its next read, and every moment of a game is one.

**The phone** (`docs/frontend.md` rule 15 "Sound"). `carriesRoom(state)` — an open room with `room.screen` false — makes `roomCues()` give a phone the screen's cues — the cheer included, its final at a phone's pace (`finalTimeline(…, 'phone')`) — as well as its own, at `SPEAKER_GAIN.phone` and under its own mute; the player fetches the room's eleven files only once it carries the room (`carry()`). Every rule of ADR 028 holds: once per moment the page saw begin, placed on the server's clock, stopped with the moment and when hidden, nothing audible about right or wrong before the reveal.
