**The cheer and the confetti on a phone** (ADR 030 and ADR 025 as amended 2026-11-19). A carrying phone says the cheer after its own fanfare, at its level times `VOICE_GAIN`; its page gets `finish` in its props, and `rooms.members.voice` passes `can:hear` (`RoomPolicy::hear`: the host or a present player of an open room) instead of `can:screen`. Its final (`FinalStanding`, `forRoom`) bursts the confetti for everyone, as the screen does. With a screen on, the screen alone cheers and a phone bursts confetti only for its winner, as before.

**Guard tests.**

| Test                                                   | What it gains                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RoomStateTest`                                        | "says the room has its screen while the screen reads, and none three beats after its last read"; "records the screen only from its own requests"; "records the screen without raising the revision, signalling or touching the room's activity, at most once a second" |
| `RoomPagesTest`                                        | the screen writes nothing but its own read; "sends the screen the room's sounds and a phone its own and the room's, and a closed room none"                                                                                                                            |
| `RoomContractTest`, `contract.test.ts`, `RoomHostTest` | the `v: 4` fixtures with `room.screen`, strict both ways                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `RoomMemberVoiceTest`, `PrivacyPageTest`               | "gives a present player's phone the clips too, and refuses one who left and a closed room"; the privacy paragraph names the players' phones                                                                                                                            |
| `sound-cues.test.ts`, `sound.test.ts`                  | "roomCues on a phone in a room without a screen" (every room cue as the screen plays it, its final at a phone's pace, and its own; the cheer only without a screen; `join`; the clock and «sent» in one moment; a screen coming on); the player's `carry()`            |

### Taunts in the middle, said aloud (T3)

**Decision** (T3, 2026-11-19; ADR 029 "Amended 2026-11-19 (T3)"). The owner asked for a better throw, a reaction in the middle of the screen with no background, slightly transparent, and a voice. Of a clickable page of variants and two auditions the owner chose: **C, «Rəqibə toxun»** — no tray, the rival's side of the strip is the button; the reaction **1, «Partlayış»**, at **85 %**; the voice **Nigar only** — the board's voice — one take per taunt; and **twelve new phrases**, emotional, real taunts and funny, two of them the owner's. Every rule of "Taunts (T2)" holds but the parts named below; nothing of the state, the protocol, the points, the window, the cooldown or what is kept changes.

**What is amended, and where the old text is.**

| Above                                                                                     | Reads from T3                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| "Taunts (T2)" — the tray beside the strip, its popover (ADR 029 decisions 17–19)          | no tray: the strip's right half is the button, with a badge on its corner; the fan of twelve opens under the strip (decisions 36–38)                                                                             |
| "Taunts (T2)" — the card over the target's strip, the screen's cards (decisions 20, 21)   | a reaction in the middle of the whole viewport, no card, 85 %, `pointer-events: none`, for 3 s (decisions 39–42)                                                                                                 |
| "Taunts (T2)" — the card pops in (decision 24)                                            | «Partlayış»: it pops in past its size, settles, and floats up as it fades (decision 43)                                                                                                                          |
| "Taunts (T2)" — no sound (decision 25); "Room games": one voice in a room (ADR 030)       | the board's voice says each taunt's phrase on its target's phone and the screen; the cheer stays the screen's — and, in a room without a screen, every phone's (ADR 030 as amended 2026-11-19) (decisions 44–48) |
| "Taunts (T2)" — the list of ten (decision 27); `taunts.json` `{key, image}` (decision 28) | twelve phrases; `taunts.json` version 2 with `voice` per taunt and a `voice` block; the prop `taunts` gains each clip's URL (decisions 49–50)                                                                    |
| "Rivals face to face (P2)" — on a phone the tray shares the strip's row (as T2 amended)   | the strip has its row's full width; ADR 027 decision 2 holds: the strip never moves for a taunt                                                                                                                  |
| "Adults' privacy" — "No contact", "Notice"                                                | a taunt is seen and heard, in a prerecorded voice, only by its target and the screen (`privacy.rooms.contact`, decision 52)                                                                                      |
| §3 "Voice" — what `content:voice` makes                                                   | one group more, `taunt`: a clip per taunt into `content/assets/rooms/taunts/`, from built-in text, never a row (decision 45)                                                                                     |

**The throw.** While a viewer who plays may throw — a live game, `taunts.open`, a rival on the strip's right, as T2 drew the tray — the strip's right half is one button (the strip's full height, `aria-label` «Atmaca at: :name», `rooms.taunts.at`, `aria-expanded`), with a 28px violet badge (`Laugh`) on the strip's bottom-right corner: muted at a balance of 0 or before the first close (a tap still opens and says why), a ring with the seconds to `ready_ms` while cooling. A dashed taunt-coloured outline shows on hover, focus and while open; the strip under it never moves. The fan under the strip: «Kimə: :name» and «1 xal», the balance or the reason, the twelve as a 4 × 3 grid (emoji 34px over the phrase, 11px, at least 64px tall), «Mənə atılanları gizlət»; one tap throws, and the fan stays open until the receipt. After a throw: «Atıldı · −1 xal» over the gap row for 2 s, «−1» rising from the badge, the badge becoming its ring.

**The reaction.** On the target's phone, a layer over the whole viewport (`position: fixed; inset: 0`, above the header and the tab bar — from F2 above the game frame's top bar), centred, with no card, background or border: the emoji (136px), the thrower's name (15px bold, the taunt's text colour) and the phrase in the page's locale (30px extra-bold, the foreground colour, at most 320px wide), all at 85 % (`--taunt-reaction-opacity`), the words haloed in the page's own background (a 6px stroke under the letters and a soft shadow). `pointer-events: none` throughout: whatever is under it is tapped through it, and nothing of the round moves or is hidden for it. It shows until `sent_ms + TAUNT_REACTION_MS` (3 000, a page constant below `show_ms`) by the estimated server clock; two stand one above the other, the older first (92px, 23px); a third waits. The screen draws `shown` the same way over its whole screen — the emoji 26vh, «:from → :to» 3.2vh, the phrase 6.4vh; two side by side, the older on the left — under its «Atmacalar» toggle. **Motion** («Partlayış», ADR 025's rules): for a reaction the page saw arrive (`tauntMoment()`), from 0.3 past 1.16 to 1 within 0.6 s, a hold, then 40px up as it fades in its last 0.6 s; two at once 0.26 s apart (`--animate-room-taunt-pop`). A page opened on one draws it still for the time left; under reduced motion it appears and goes, and «−1» is not drawn.

**The voice.** One clip per taunt, made by `content:voice --only=taunt` (§3 "Voice"): Nigar — `voice.json`'s voice, `eleven_v4`, `az` — saying the taunt's Azerbaijani phrase after its own tag, `[mischievously] Tısbağa da səndən tezdir!`, with the taunts' own context; −16 LUFS; `content/assets/rooms/taunts/{hash}.mp3`, listed under `voice-clips.json`'s `taunts`, credited (MIT, "Elifba contributors") from `taunts.json`'s `voice.credit`. The same clip in every locale and whatever anyone answered. **Who hears it**: the devices that draw the reaction — the target's phone and the screen; never the thrower, never another phone, and in a room without a screen only the target's phone (`carriesRoom()` carries the room's cues, never a taunt). «Mənə atılanları gizlət» and the screen's «Atmacalar» silence it with the reaction, the device's mute silences the voice alone, the host's switch off silences everything. **When**: a `roomCues()` cue, `{sound: 'taunt', id, key}`, once for a taunt of `received` (a phone) or `shown` (the screen) the page saw arrive — never for a page opened on it, a state fetched again, a reaction drawn late or already over; it starts with the reaction and is not cut by the round's close, and it stops (fading in `STOP_MS`) when the page is hidden, the game ends or the room closes; one at a time on a device, a second waiting for the first and dropped if its reaction is over by then. **How loud**: `SPEAKER_GAIN` (the screen 1, a phone 0.5) × `VOICE_GAIN`, like the cheer. **Fetching**: the prop's URLs (about 300 KB for twelve), once the device's sound is unlocked and on and the room's taunts are on, decoded and kept in memory; on iOS audio is made in the first gesture (`unlock()`), so an untouched phone draws the reaction in silence.

**The list** (`content/rooms/taunts.json`, version 2; the phrases `rooms.taunts.say.{key}`; ADR 029 decision 49 has the English and each `voice` tag): 🐢 «Tısbağa da səndən tezdir!», ⏰ «Tik-tak, vaxt gedir ha!», 🥶 «Dondun qaldın, nə oldu?», 😴 «Oyan, oyan! Oyun gedir!», 🍀 «Vay, bəxtin necə gətirdi!», 🎲 «Zər atırsan, nədir?», 😎 «Bu raund mənimdir, ha-ha!», 🚀 «Yetiş görüm, yetişə bilsən!», 🥱 «Pəh! Nə asan idi!», 😅 «Vay-vay, az qaldı! Heyif!», 🤪 «Ay lölüy!» (`silly`, `1F92A.svg`), 😂 «Noldu, Paşinyan?!» (`what_now`, `1F602.svg`). Each taunt is `{key, image, voice}`; the file has a `voice` block, `{context, credit}`. The validator adds: a `voice` (`^[a-z]{3,24}$`) on every taunt, the block's `context` and credit, no other key, every clip under `taunts` present under `assets/rooms/taunts/`, an MP3 of at most 48 KB and some taunt's, no file there outside the list, the folder credited once it holds one. The prop `taunts` is `[{key, url, voice}]`, `voice` the clip's URL or null (a taunt not voiced yet is silent). `what_now` names a public figure by the owner's explicit choice over this step's advice (ADR 029 decision 49); like every phrase it changes as content.

**Unchanged.** The state (`v: 4`), its DTOs, schemas and fixtures; the channel; the routes, tables and config (`show_ms` stays 4 000); the cost, the balance, the window, the cooldown, the host's switch and what is kept; the room's sound set (`sounds.json`, `RoomSound`). `/privacy` changes one clause of `privacy.rooms.contact` (ADR 029 decision 52); one interface key is new, `rooms.taunts.at`.

**Guard tests** (ADR 029 decision 54).

| Test                                   | What it gains                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `RoomTauntsTest`, `RoomPagesTest`      | the twelve keys and tags pinned; a phrase per taunt in both languages and no other; every approved taunt's clip listed with its text and present; the pages' `taunts[].voice`, and a closed room none; `TAUNT_REACTION_MS` below `show_ms`                                                                                     |
| `GenerateVoiceTest`                    | a taunt's clip from its az phrase, its tag and the taunts' context, into `content/assets/rooms/taunts`; `--only=taunt`; `--prune`; the credits entry with the first clip; no row read                                                                                                                                          |
| `ContentValidatorTest`                 | one broken fixture per rule of `taunts.json` version 2 and its clips                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `sound-cues.test.ts`, `taunts.test.ts` | a taunt said once, as the page saw it arrive, on the target's phone and the screen only; never for a page opened on it, a fetched-again state, the thrower or another phone, with a screen or without; silent while hidden, toggled off or muted; one at a time; the reaction until `sent_ms + TAUNT_REACTION_MS`, two stacked |
| `RoomsApartTest`, `PrivacyPageTest`    | a taunt's clip only by its prop's URL (`assets/rooms/taunts`, beside `fluent/`); "says a taunt is seen and heard only by its target and the big screen"                                                                                                                                                                        |

**Why.** The strip is where the rival is, so the throw is the rival's own name, and the strip keeps its width. A taunt is meant to sting: in the middle, big, and said — yet it never takes a tap or moves the question, and it goes in 3 s. One voice, the board's, keeps the room sounding like Elifba; one clip per taunt is what the owner chose by ear, and it needs no rule to keep two devices in step.

**Rejected.** A, «Baş barmaq» (the round's bottom row narrowed) and B, «Reaksiya paneli» (the tab bar hidden, the words unseen until a throw lands); the motions «Möhür» and «Uçub gəlir»; 100 % and 70 %; the three default ElevenLabs voices; several takes per taunt picked from its id; an English clip; the thrower or every phone hearing it; speech at run time (ADR 029 "Alternatives rejected (T3)").

**As built (T4, 2026-11-19; ADR 029 "As built (T4)").** As above, with: the fan also closes when the round's phase changes, so a tap meant for the next question never lands on a tile or on «Mənə atılanları gizlət» (the suspected cause of the iPhone→Android report, for which no delivery bug was found); the reaction is a portal into `document.body` with the `taunt-halo` utility, and its pop runs the time it has left to `sent_ms + TAUNT_REACTION_MS` (at least 600 ms, `tauntPops()`); the cue carries `until`, the ms to its reaction's end, `roomCues()` takes the device's `{tauntsHidden}` and reads taunts only while `tauntsLive()`, and the player says them outside the moment's cues (`voices()`, `taunt()`, `hush()`); `content:voice --only=taunt` builds its lines in `Content\Support\TauntVoices` (twelve clips, 443 characters, 226 KB); `privacy.rooms.contact_voice` (shown only with the live voice on) keeps the old clause, since ADR 031 decision 16 pins it — the voice's release changes both; the state is `v: 5` since C3 and T4 changes none of it.

### Live voice (C2–C5)

**Decision** (C1, 2026-11-18; ADR 031). Players of a room can talk while they play, through a voice server the instance hosts itself, behind a switch of its own. The owner decided the frame that day — our own server, never a cloud service and never phone to phone; off in a room until the host turns it on; each player opens their own microphone; the host mutes anyone; never recorded. The variant of the pages (A, «Zolaq»), the newcomer rule and the sentences are C1's recommendation among three clickable prototypes, which the owner reads before the switch is turned on (C5-O; ADR 031's status line). This subsection is the contract of C2 (the voice server in Docker), C3 (the server and the state), C4 (the pages and the words) and C5 (hardening and audit); until a step lands, nothing of what it is marked with exists. Nothing below loosens a rule of a room: every player is still an account shown under `users.name`, a code still admits and grants nothing else, nothing is typed, the server alone judges, scores and keeps the clock, and the room channel still carries `room.changed` and nothing else. **Amended 2026-11-19 (voice on by default; ADR 031 decisions 35–43, the owner's request):** a room's voice is on from its opening and the host turns it off and on; an admitted player whose account agreed is in it, listening, without a tap; a player who comes in before a game is let in at once, one who comes in during a game waits; the limit counts voice rooms in use; and a page captures the microphone at its owner's first «Mikrofonu aç» and keeps it, closed, until its player leaves the voice, so the browser asks at most once per page. The text below reads as amended; "As amended (voice on by default)" at the end of this subsection has what the build changes.

**What is amended, and where the old text is.**

| Above                                                                                                              | From   | Reads                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| "The race" — The state: `v: 1` (`v: 2` from G2, `v: 3` from T2, `v: 4` from "A room without a screen")             | C3     | `v: 5`, with `voice` and each player's `voice` ("The state's voice" below); `room.changed` stays `{v: 1, revision}`                                                                                                                                                                 |
| "The race" — The channel: "no client event means anything in a room: the pages send none"                          | C4     | unchanged for the room channel; a page in the voice holds a second connection, to the voice server, which carries audio and that server's own signalling and no game fact                                                                                                           |
| "Rooms and joining" — The host's tools; the limiters                                                               | C3     | three more routes ("Routes" below); `room-voice` (12 a minute per account)                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| "Adults' privacy" — "No contact", "Minimum data", "Notice"                                                         | C3, C4 | as amended in that table                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ADR 025 decision 11 and docs/frontend.md rule 15 "Sound": "The screen is the room's speaker"                       | C4     | the speaker of the room's cues; voices are heard on each player's own phone, and the screen plays, captures and joins no voice                                                                                                                                                      |
| docs/frontend.md rule 15 "Nothing more": "No voice" (ADR 030: "No spoken voice but the winner's cheer")            | C4     | rule 15 "Live voice" for people's own voices; still no speech synthesis, and no recorded voice but ADR 030's cheer at the final                                                                                                                                                     |
| ADR 030 (the winner's cheer): "one voice enters a room … and so does every other voice"; its `privacy.rooms.voice` | C3, C4 | of the voices a room plays; people's live voice is ADR 031's, on their phones, never the screen's. The live voice's part of `/privacy` is `privacy.rooms.live_voice_title` and `privacy.rooms.live_voice`; C4 renames the «Səs» button in `privacy.rooms.voice` with the cue toggle |
| §10: "the only published ports" (the proxy's)                                                                      | C2     | with the `voice` profile, also the voice server's two media ports ("Live voice" in §10)                                                                                                                                                                                             |

**The switch** (C3). `config('elifba.rooms.voice.enabled')` ← `ELIFBA_ROOMS_VOICE`, false unless set; `App\Support\RoomsArea::voice()` is its one reader and is true only while the area is on (`RoomsArea::enabled()`), the switch is set, and `elifba.rooms.voice.{key, secret, url, api_url}` are there. While it is false: the middleware `rooms.voice` (`EnsureRoomVoiceIsEnabled`) answers 404 on every voice route; the state's `voice` is null; `/privacy` and the welcome page say nothing of voice; no page asks for a ticket, and `livekit-client` is never loaded. The columns exist either way. An instance without the Compose profile `voice` runs today's services and nothing else.

**The facts** (C3; migration `add_voice_to_rooms`, additive and nullable; §1 "Tables" gains them with the step):

| Column                           | Written by                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Is                                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `rooms.voice_at`                 | `OpenRoom` (while the instance's voice is on; as amended 2026-11-19), `SetRoomVoice`; cleared by it, `CloseRoom` and `CloseIdleRooms`                                                                        | the room's voice is on since this moment; set in every room opened while `RoomsArea::voice()`, null in one opened without it |
| `rooms.voice_epoch`              | `SetRoomVoice` (off), `RemoveRoomPlayer`, `CloseRoom`, `SweepRoomVoices`                                                                                                                                     | a small counter in the voice room's name; raising it closes the voice room for everyone                                      |
| `room_players.voice_admitted_at` | `OpenRoom` (the host's row) and `JoinRoom` (a row that comes in while the voice is on and no game runs), as amended 2026-11-19; `SetRoomVoice` (on: every present player and the host), `SetRoomPlayerVoice` | let into the voice; null: waiting                                                                                            |
| `room_players.voice_muted_at`    | `SetRoomPlayerVoice`                                                                                                                                                                                         | muted by the host, until the host lifts it                                                                                   |
| `users.voice_agreed_at`          | `JoinRoomVoice`                                                                                                                                                                                              | the account accepted the voice's sentence (§7 "Agreements")                                                                  |

A player's standing — `waiting`, `muted`, `open` (`Rooms\Enums\RoomVoiceStanding`) — is derived by `Rooms\Support\RoomVoice::standingOf()` and never stored; the host's row is always `open`. Who joined the voice, whose microphone is open and who speaks are stored nowhere.

**The actions** (C3; only they write, each under the room row's lock, raising the revision and signalling through `RoomSignal`):

- `Rooms\Actions\OpenRoom` (as amended 2026-11-19, ADR 031 decision 37) — told by its controller whether the instance's voice is on (`RoomsArea::voice()`, as `RoomState::for(…, voice:)` is told), it sets `voice_at` on a new room and `voice_admitted_at` on the host's row; it makes no call to the voice server (the first ticket makes the voice room). The host's open room taking another game (`OpenRoom::choose()`) leaves the voice as it is.
- `Rooms\Actions\SetRoomVoice` — `PUT /rooms/{room}/voice` `{on}` (`Rooms\Data\RoomVoiceSwitchData::rules()`). On: sets `voice_at` (it refused with `rooms.voice.errors.busy` while `elifba.rooms.voice.max_rooms` other open rooms had their voice on until 2026-11-19; the limit is now the ticket's); stamps `voice_admitted_at` on the host's row and on every present player's that has none. Off: clears `voice_at`, raises the epoch. It changes and signals nothing when the room already is where asked. Allowed in every phase.
- `Rooms\Actions\SetRoomPlayerVoice` — `PUT /rooms/{room}/members/{member}/voice` `{standing: open | muted}` (`RoomPlayerVoiceData::rules()`): stamps the admission if there is none and sets or clears `voice_muted_at`. Never the host's row (`RoomPolicy::voice` with the member, as `remove` refuses it).
- `Rooms\Actions\JoinRoomVoice` — `POST /rooms/{room}/voice/tickets` `{agree?}` (`JoinRoomVoiceData::rules()`: `agree` must be accepted while the account has no `voice_agreed_at`): stamps the agreement, asks the voice server to make the voice room if it is not there, and returns the ticket (`RoomVoiceTicketData`). As amended 2026-11-19 (ADR 031 decision 38) it first lists the voice rooms (`ListRooms`, with each one's participants): while its own voice room has nobody in it and `max_rooms` other voice rooms have someone, it refuses with 503 `{status: "busy"}` (`Rooms\Exceptions\RoomVoiceBusy`) and stamps nothing. It writes nothing of the room, raises no revision and is not a read (`RecordRoomRead` never runs for it).
- `Rooms\Actions\SweepRoomVoices` — `rooms:voice-sweep`, every 15 seconds ("Enforcement" below).
- `JoinRoom` (as amended 2026-11-19, ADR 031 decision 39) stamps `voice_admitted_at` on a row that has none while the room's voice is on and no game is running (`runningGame()` null: the start screen, between games, the final); an account that joins while a game runs waits for «Səsə burax»; a player who returns keeps what their row had. `RemoveRoomPlayer` raises the epoch while the voice is on, for a row let into the voice (C5: a newcomer who waits holds no ticket, so removing one cuts nobody). `LeaveRoom`, `LockRoom`, `SetHostPlays` write nothing of voice.

**Routes** (the player group and `rooms.voice`; `{room}` and `{member}` as everywhere):

| Method and path                            | Reaches it (policy)                                                                                        | Answer                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `PUT /rooms/{room}/voice`                  | `voice`: the host of an open room                                                                          | `SetRoomVoice`; 204 (422 `busy` until 2026-11-19)                                                                                    |
| `PUT /rooms/{room}/members/{member}/voice` | `voice` with the member: a row of that room that is not the host's                                         | `SetRoomPlayerVoice`; 204                                                                                                            |
| `POST /rooms/{room}/voice/tickets`         | `listen`: the host, or a present admitted player, of an open room whose voice is on; `throttle:room-voice` | `{url, token}` (JSON, `no-store`) / 422 `agreement` / 503 `{status: "unavailable"}` / 503 `{status: "busy"}` (as amended 2026-11-19) |

They are guarded by the policy and by nothing about the page; `rooms/screen` calls none of them.

**The state's voice** (C3). `RoomStateData::VERSION` becomes 5 — one above the 4 of "A room without a screen" (`room.screen`), which reached `main` first; taunts took 3, and C1 had written 3:

```
RoomState { v: 5, …,                                                   // every other key as in "The state", "The state's choice", "Taunts (T2)" and "A room without a screen"
  voice:   { on: bool, agreed: bool } | null,                          // null: RoomsArea::voice() is false, or a closed room
  players: [{ id, name | null, present, host,
              voice: open | muted | waiting | null }] }                // not null exactly for the host's row and the present players' while voice.on
```

`Rooms\Data\RoomVoiceData` is the state's key and `RoomPlayerData` gains `voice`; `RoomVoice`, a strict object, and the player's `voice` in `resources/js/rooms/contract.ts`. `agreed` is the viewer's own account's. Fixtures: every `state.*.json` gains `voice: null` and each player's `voice: null`; `state.voice.json` (the start screen, the voice on, the viewer agreed; one player `open`, one `muted`, one `waiting`) and `state.voice.off.json` (the host's view, `on` false) are new. A page that meets a `v` it does not know reloads itself once, as at G2.

**The ticket** (C3). `Rooms\Support\RoomVoiceTicket::for($room, $player)`: a JWT signed HS256 with `elifba.rooms.voice.secret` through `firebase/php-jwt` (which C3 names in `composer.json`; it is already installed through Reverb) — `iss` the key, `sub` the player's `room_players.id`, `nbf` now, `exp` now + `ticket_seconds` (60), and `video`: `room` `{rooms.id}.{voice_epoch}`, `roomJoin` true, `canSubscribe` true, `canPublish` true only for an `open` standing, `canPublishData` false, `canPublishSources` `["microphone"]`, `canUpdateOwnMetadata` false. Every permission is written out, because the voice server grants what a token leaves out; `name`, `metadata` and `attributes` are never set, nor `roomCreate` (which would defeat `auto_create: false`), `roomAdmin`, `roomList`, `roomRecord`, `hidden`, `recorder`, `agent` or `ingressAdmin`. The answer's `url` is `elifba.rooms.voice.url`. `exp` bounds only the join: the voice server keeps a connected client's token fresh by itself, which is why a removal is an epoch and not a call (below).

**Enforcement at the voice server** (C3). `Rooms\Support\RoomVoiceServer` is the only class that talks to it — Laravel's HTTP client against `elifba.rooms.voice.api_url`, the Twirp JSON calls `/twirp/livekit.RoomService/{CreateRoom, DeleteRoom, RemoveParticipant, UpdateParticipant, ListRooms, ListParticipants}`, each with a token minted for that call (30 seconds; `roomCreate`, or `roomAdmin` with the room, or `roomList`), connect and answer within `timeout_ms` (1 500). `Rooms\Support\RoomVoiceCut` makes the calls an action owes, in `DB::afterCommit`, after `RoomSignal`'s broadcast, never inside the transaction or under the room's lock:

| The fact                                                                                                                    | The call                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| the voice turned on                                                                                                         | `CreateRoom` `{name, empty_timeout: 300, departure_timeout: 20, max_participants: max_players + 1}`                                                                                                                      |
| a player muted, or let speak again                                                                                          | `UpdateParticipant` with the whole permission set (`can_subscribe` true, `can_publish`, `can_publish_data` false, `can_publish_sources` `["MICROPHONE"]`, `hidden` false, `recorder` false); "not found" is not an error |
| a player let into the voice removed (a newcomer who waits holds no ticket: none, C5); the voice turned off; the room closed | `DeleteRoom` of the name with the epoch before the raise                                                                                                                                                                 |
| a player left                                                                                                               | `RemoveParticipant`                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| a lock, an admission, the host stepping out of playing                                                                      | none                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

A call that fails or times out never fails the write; it is reported as `Rooms\Exceptions\RoomVoiceNotCut` with the room id and the cause's class, as `RoomChangeNotBroadcast` is. `SweepRoomVoices` finishes what a call missed and catches what no call covers — a room gone idle (`Room::isOpen()`), a deleted account, the instance's switch going off, a ticket replayed by a changed client: it deletes a voice room whose room is not open, has its voice off or has another epoch, and where it finds a participant who may not be there, or a muted player holding the permission to publish, it raises the epoch and deletes the voice room — but for a player's own leave or mute of the last `ticket_seconds` whose participant joined before it (the voice server's `joined_at_ms`, two seconds of slack), which gets that fact's call again ("As built (C3)", as C5 amended it). It is scheduled while a voice server is configured (key, secret and `api_url`; in Docker `api_url` is set only while `ELIFBA_ROOMS_VOICE_IP` is, §10 "Live voice", so an instance without the profile schedules none and never calls `voice:7880`), the switch on or off, and with the switch off deletes every voice room. It touches no round, no clock and no `seen_ms`.

**A voice server that is down delays nothing.** `RecordRoomAnswer`, `RecordRoomRead`, `RoomState`, `StartRoomGame`, `EndRoomGame` and `ChooseRoomGame` make no request to it; `LeaveRoom` and `RemoveRoomPlayer` commit their round's close and signal before theirs. Tests fake it with `tests/Support/FakeVoiceServer` (`Http::fake` on `api_url`: `->sent`, `->down()`), as `FakeReverb` fakes the broadcaster, and never reach a real one.

**The page** (C4; as amended 2026-11-19). `resources/js/rooms/voice.ts` is the only file that imports `livekit-client` — dynamically, when the page first joins the voice (by itself for an admitted player whose account agreed, or on the tap of «Səsə qoşul»), so it is its own chunk from the app's host. It connects with `rtcConfig: { iceServers: [] }` always (the voice server's own list is never used), `audioCaptureDefaults` `{echoCancellation, noiseSuppression, autoGainControl: true}`, `publishDefaults` `{audioPreset: AudioPresets.speech, dtx: true, red: true, stopMicTrackOnMute: false}` and `stopLocalTrackOnUnpublish: false` (C4 had `stopMicTrackOnMute: true`), no data channel use, no encryption option. The microphone is off at every join; it is captured at its owner's first «Mikrofonu aç» on the page and kept — closed by disabling the track, opened again by enabling it, with no new `getUserMedia` — until the page leaves the voice («Səsdən çıx», the voice off, a removal, a close, the page left) or the host mutes the player, which release it; a voice room closed under the page publishes the same track again. It is opened only by the player's tap, closed by the page when the page is hidden, and restored only when the page itself comes back into a voice room that was closed under it. `voiceView(state, live)` (`resources/js/rooms/voice-view.ts`, Vitest) decides what is drawn from the state and from what the voice connection says; nothing else reads the voice connection. `rooms/screen` reaches no voice connection: its imports reach neither `rooms/voice.ts`, `components/rooms/use-room-voice.ts`, `components/rooms/voice-bar.tsx` nor the library, and it draws the state's facts through `screenVoiceView(state)` of the same pure `voice-view.ts`, which imports nothing of the connection (C4, "As built (C4)" below). docs/frontend.md rule 15 "Live voice" has the pages.

**Who hears where; in which phases; failures; targets.** ADR 031 decisions 25, 26 and 31 are the contract, and C5 measures decision 31's table.
