**As built (C3).** Settled before the code (2026-11-19), on `main` with taunts:

- The state is `v: 5`, since taunts took 3 and a room without a screen 4 ("The state's voice" above; ADR 031 "The state").
- `privacy.rooms.contact_voice` is `privacy.rooms.contact` as taunts reworded it, with the voice's clauses (ADR 031 decision 16); "Adults' privacy" — "No contact" names both a taunt and the voice.
- `rooms.voice` (`EnsureRoomVoiceIsEnabled`) stands right after `rooms.enabled` in the middleware priority list: with the voice off a voice route is a 404 before the cookies, the session or the login could say it exists — to a visitor too, as the rooms switch is.
- The host's ticket names the host's own row, which every room opened since G2 has (`OpenRoom`), whether the host plays or stepped out; `listen` refuses a host without one.
- `SetRoomPlayerVoice` changes nothing on a removed row; it may admit or mute a player who left, whose row keeps it for a return. Its call to the voice server goes out only while the room's voice is on.
- `SweepRoomVoices` raises an epoch under the room's lock and raises no revision: the state carries no epoch, and the pages learn of it from the voice server, which disconnects them.
- The schedule: routes/console.php calls `SweepRoomVoices::schedule()`, which registers `rooms:voice-sweep` every 15 seconds only while the key, the secret and `api_url` are set.

Learned while building it (2026-11-19):

- **The domain is told the switch.** `Rooms` names nothing of the app outside its list (`RoomsApartTest`), so it never calls `RoomsArea::voice()`: the room controllers pass it to `RoomState::for(…, voice:)`, the command passes it to `SweepRoomVoices::handle()`, and the policy's `voice` and `listen` rely on `rooms.voice`, which every voice route carries. `RoomsArea::voice()` stays the one reader of the switch.
- **What the voice server answers**, run once against `composer dev:voice` (LiveKit v1.13.11) and copied into `tests/Support/FakeVoiceServer`: a body must be a JSON object — `ListRooms` with `[]` is 400 `malformed`, so the app sends `{"names": []}`; unknown fields are dropped; `CreateRoom` of a room that exists answers that room; `DeleteRoom` needs `roomCreate` (`roomAdmin` with the room is 401) and answers 404 `not_found` for a room that is gone; `RemoveParticipant` and `UpdateParticipant` answer 404 "participant does not exist", even in a room that is gone; `ListParticipants` of a room that is gone is an empty list; a participant's `name` is empty and its `permission` lists every flag. `UpdateParticipant` also sends `can_update_metadata: false`, the ticket's `canUpdateOwnMetadata`.
- **The try against the real thing** (outside the repository, `~/elifba-shots/voice/c3/`: `app.php` boots the worktree's `RoomVoiceServer` and `RoomVoiceTicket`, `harness/try.mjs` drives two headless Chromes): the voice room `CreateRoom` made took both tickets; the muted ticket's publish was refused ("insufficient permissions") while it heard the other; `UpdateParticipant` without `can_publish` unpublished the open player's microphone at once (permissions changed, then the local track unpublished); `DeleteRoom` disconnected both with `ROOM_DELETED` within 0.1 s. The voice server's log held the row ids and the voice rooms' names, and no ticket.
- `JoinRoomVoice` reads the room again after `CreateRoom` and asks once more when the epoch moved meanwhile; a voice room it made for an old epoch is the sweep's. `RoomVoiceUnreachable` renders its own 503. `SetRoomVoice` marks the room active, as the taunts' switch does; `SetRoomPlayerVoice` does not, as a removal does not. Tickets and the app's call tokens take their moments from the app's clock.
- `firebase/php-jwt` is named in `composer.json` (`^7.2`); the lock keeps v7.2.1.
- **Beside a room without a screen** (ADR 028 as amended 2026-11-19, on `main` the same night): its `room.screen` took the state's `v: 4`, so the voice's is `v: 5`; and since every phone then plays the room's cues, ADR 031 decision 25 says a phone in the voice keeps the cues it plays, the room's ones included. The screen's own reads (`RecordScreenRead`) and the voice never meet: the screen still joins no voice.
- **Beside the winner's cheer** (ADR 030, on `main` the same night): the live voice's ADR is 031. The cheer's route `GET /rooms/{room}/members/{member}/voice` (`rooms.members.voice`, `RoomMemberVoiceController`, `can:screen`) shares its path with the host's `PUT` (`rooms.members.voice.update`), which is not a clash of method and path; the host's controller is `RoomPlayerVoiceController`, since the cheer's took the name first. The cheer's route is outside `rooms.voice`: it plays a recorded name and is no part of the live voice.
- **The sweep repeats a fresh cut instead of cutting everyone** (the review of C3; ADR 031 decision 23 as built). Between an action's commit and its own call to the voice server — the broadcast first, up to its timeout, then the call — a player who just left is still listed, and a ticket signed a moment before a mute can join after the mute's `UpdateParticipant` missed it. So within `ticket_seconds` (60) of the player's own `left_at` or `voice_muted_at` the sweep makes that call itself (`RemoveParticipant`, or `UpdateParticipant` without the microphone) and raises nothing; later, the same sight is a replayed ticket and raises the epoch as decided. A player the host let speak who is listed without the microphone gets it back (`UpdateParticipant` with it), since that grants nothing the facts do not. The sweep lists the participants before it reads the rows, so an admission or a «let speak» that a ticket followed is always seen. **C5 (the audit)** narrowed the repeat to a participant who joined before the fact, or within two seconds after it (`JOIN_SLACK_MS`; the facts are stored to the second): `RoomVoiceServer::participants()` reads each participant's `joined_at_ms`, which a resumed session keeps and a new join renews. One who joined later came back with a ticket or a refreshed token from before the fact — the voice server refreshes a connected client's token with the grants of that moment, valid ten minutes, and a changed client may keep it — and gets the new epoch at the first sweep that sees it, where C3's rule called it again at every sweep for the whole minute (60–75 s heard, in the audit's harness).
- **`JoinRoomVoice` and a fact that lands while it waits**: when the room's voice room name moved, its voice went off or the room closed between its `CreateRoom` and its second read, the voice room it made is deleted again at once (the action's own `DeleteRoom` may have come first), and the ticket is for the voice room as it is now, or refused.
- **The voice room's timeouts**, as LiveKit means them: `empty_timeout: 300` closes a voice room nobody joined after five minutes; `departure_timeout: 20` closes one 20 seconds after the last participant left — not five minutes, as ADR 031 decision 21's prose had it — and the next ticket makes it again.
- **`max_rooms`** is counted under the room's own lock only, so two hosts turning their voice on at the same instant may make it six; accepted, since it bounds the voice server's load and grants nothing.
- **`RoomVoiceNotCut`** names the room by id — `*` for a failed listing of the sweep — and the cause by its class or, for an answer that is an error, its HTTP status.

**As built (C4, words; 2026-11-19).** The first of C4's two sessions put every word in place and drew nothing of a room; the room pages, the screen and `livekit-client` are the second's (C4b).

- `lang/az.json` and `lang/en.json` hold ADR 031's texts word for word — the table "Words", decisions 14–17 and decision 25: 57 new keys a language (`rooms.voice.*`, `welcome.rooms.names_voice`, `privacy.rooms.{contact,not_stored,where}_voice`, `privacy.rooms.live_voice_title`, `privacy.rooms.live_voice`) and three values changed (`rooms.sound.label` «Oyun səsləri», `rooms.sound.unlock` «Oyun səslərini aç», and the button's name in `privacy.rooms.voice`). `RoomsWordingTest` reads them from the ADR itself, so a sentence changes there first.
- **The cue toggle's new name does not wait for the switch.** `rooms.sound.label` and `.unlock` are the visible text of the screen's toggle (`components/rooms/sound-toggle.tsx`, unchanged), so an instance with the voice off also reads «Oyun səsləri» — the one difference it sees; `/privacy` names the button the same way.
- `privacy.rooms.live_voice` keeps its five paragraphs as five lines, as `privacy.rooms.stored` keeps its lines; the page draws a `<p>` a line in the part «Canlı səs» (`id="live-voice"`, an inset under its own `h3`) after `where` and before the operator, who still closes the section — below the contact part, which points to it. `VOICE_FORMS` in `pages/privacy.tsx` swaps `contact`, `not_stored` and `where` for their voice forms; both swaps read only `rooms.voice`. The welcome section reads `voice` from its own prop.
- `TranslationsTest` "keeps each agreement its own sentence" counts `rooms.voice.agreement` among the agreements: never equal to, inside or around a role's.
- Both READMEs' sections for adults' games gain a bullet on the live voice and its switch, and their privacy paragraphs say that the voice is not recorded.

**As built (C4, pages; 2026-11-19).** The second session drew the voice as rule 15 "Live voice" places it, on the words above.

- **Files.** `resources/js/rooms/voice.ts` (`VoiceLink`: `join()`, `setMic()`, `leave()`, `unlockAudio()`, `dispose()`; `loadVoiceLibrary()`, the one `import('livekit-client')`; `roomOptions()`, `connectOptions()`, `REJOIN_MS` = 0, 1 000 and 3 000 ms; the ticket's schema), `resources/js/rooms/voice-view.ts` (`voiceView()`, `screenVoiceView()`, `myVoiceRow()`; the types `VoiceConnection` and `VoiceLive` that `voice.ts` reports in), `components/rooms/use-room-voice.ts` (`useRoomVoice()`: the link made at the first «Səsə qoşul», the ticket request, the notes, the host's writes) and `components/rooms/voice-bar.tsx` (`VoiceBar`, `VoiceDock`, `VoiceConsent`, `VoiceSheet`, `VoiceSwitchRow`, `useVoiceSwitch()`, `SpeakingBars`); `RoomActionBar` takes the bar above its action (`voice`), `RoomTools` takes «Canlı səs» as its first row (`voice`) and `SwitchRow` an icon; the tokens `--voice`, `--voice-text`, `--voice-on`, `--voice-tint` and the keyframes `--animate-room-voice-bar` in `app.css`. `livekit-client` 2.24.1 is in `package.json` exactly and is built as its own chunk (578 kB, 153 kB gzipped), fetched from the app's host at the first «Səsə qoşul» and never before (§3 "Stack", "Extras").
- **The screen reads `voice-view.ts`.** The contract's "imports no voice module" and the guard test's "gives the screen only the state's facts" are one rule: the screen draws `screenVoiceView(state)` — the voice on, and each waiting or muted player's mark — from the pure module, and its imports reach no connection, no ticket and no library (`RoomsApartTest`, followed through every import).
- **Fit** (measured before the build at 375 × 667: the 4 × 4 grid was 211px, the row under it ended at 566px and the 8-letter anagram's «Geri» at 551px, while the bar's top stands at 546px). While the bar shows, the round's page has 0.5rem less padding above and the round's three parts stand 0.75rem apart (`LiveRound` `compact`), and the grid's limit is `100svh − 28.5rem − var(--room-voice)` with `--room-voice: 2.5rem` (the bar's 3.5rem less the 1rem given back): a 4 × 4 grid keeps cells of 44px, its floor, with its row under it ending at about 539px, and the anagram's «Geri» at 535px — both above the bar without scrolling. The 5 × 5 and 6 × 6 grids, which scrolled before, scroll 2.5rem more. With the voice off nothing of this applies: `--room-voice` is unset and the classes are as they were.
- **What the build says beyond the table.** The sheet «Canlı səs» has `rooms.voice.count` under its title (no key holds «· səs yazılmır»); the waiting form is the one sentence `rooms.voice.waiting_you`; a dropped connection says `rooms.voice.errors.unreachable` with «Yenidən qoşul»; a note — the hidden page's microphone, «Aparıcı sizi susdurub», a refused or missing microphone, «Aparıcı canlı səsi söndürdü.» — is a line above the bar for 6 s (`NOTE_MS`), and the last one stays alone where the bar was; the host sees one newcomer's line at a time, and every waiting row in the sheet. The sheet opens from the bar in the voice, so a host who has not joined admits from the line and turns the voice off in «Otağın idarəsi» outside a round. The microphone's pill is named by its word, `aria-pressed` while open, with `rooms.voice.mic.open` / `.close` as its title.
- **The connection.** A voice room closed under the page — `ROOM_DELETED`, and `ROOM_CLOSED` or `PARTICIPANT_REMOVED` the same way — asks for a ticket at once and after 1 and 3 s more, then says it could not join; a refused ticket leaves the page out of the voice, and the state says why. `DUPLICATE_IDENTITY` is «Canlı səs başqa cihazınızda açıqdır.». The library leaves the voice with the page (`disconnectOnPageLeave`, which also covers a frozen tab), and the page comes back in when it is shown again. The microphone the page restores after a voice room was closed under it is only one its owner had open a moment before; the host's mute closes it for good (decision 10). Heard voices play through hidden `<audio>` elements the library attaches; `startAudio()` runs after the join, and a browser that still holds them back (iOS Safari) plays them from the next tap anywhere on the page.
- **Seen in a browser** (`composer dev:voice`, LiveKit v1.13.11, the stack outside the repository in `~/elifba-shots/voice/c4b/`: headless Chrome, a Web Audio tone as each microphone, every `RTCPeerConnection` recorded): every connection was built with `iceServers: []`; each page asked only its own host, Reverb and the voice server; from «Razıyam, qoşul» to hearing the host 340 ms; a microphone opened to heard 344 ms; the host's mute to silence 255 ms; a removal to the removed player's cut 56 ms, and the others heard again 385 ms after it; the voice off to silence 257 ms; a hidden page's microphone stopped within 2 ms. With the voice server killed in a game every answer was taken and every round closed; the bar said «Səs kəsildi, yenidən qoşulur…» and, when the library gave up, «Yenidən qoşul». With the switch off the room pages, the screen and «Otağın idarəsi» drew the same pixels as before C4b at 375 × 667 and 1280 × 720 (the round's boxes equal, its time bar aside) and made the same requests but for the scripts' chunks.

**As amended (the head's button, 2026-11-19).** After the first day on elifba.io the owner found the bar too big and its «Sönülü» a label: ADR 031 decision 34 and docs/frontend.md rule 15 "Live voice" now draw one round button in the page's head (`VoiceButton`) and the panel «Canlı səs» it opens (`VoicePanel`), in `components/rooms/voice-bar.tsx`, from the same `voice.view`. The bar's "Fit" above no longer applies: the button takes no height, and `--room-voice`, `LiveRound`'s `compact` and `RoomActionBar`'s `voice` are gone. Nothing of the state, the routes, the server or the words changed.

**As built (C5, measurements; 2026-11-19).** C5's first session measured ADR 031 decision 31 and ran decision 26's drills; the decision's table now carries the harness's number beside each target, and its "As built (C5, measurements)" says how each was taken. The harness lives outside the repository, as `rooms-flow` keeps its own (`~/elifba-shots/voice/c5/`: `measure.md`, the scripts, every run's JSON).

- **Two changes, each with its test.** `VoiceLink::join()` asks for the ticket while the library loads instead of after it (`resources/js/rooms/voice.ts`; `voice.test.ts` above): a device's first «Səsə qoşul» on a slow mobile link 3.43 → 3.10 s. `RemoveRoomPlayer` raises the epoch only for a row with `voice_admitted_at` — a newcomer who waits never had a ticket, and `voice_admitted_at` is never cleared — so removing one cuts nobody (`RoomVoiceCutTest` above; ADR 031 decision 22's table, and the table of `RoomVoiceCut` above).
- **Three targets changed** in decision 31 with the measurements beside them: the first tap of a device, which downloads the library (decision 1 keeps it behind the tap); and the two bandwidth rows, written before RED's two redundant frames per frame were counted. RED stays: at 5 % loss it cut the concealed share from 4.5 % to 1.0 %.
- **Learned.** The 15 s after which a half-open signalling socket is given up is the voice server's ping timeout, fixed in LiveKit v1.13.11 (no configuration names it); until then the page draws the voice as it was. Each epoch a page meets costs it a ticket, as does a voice turned off (one refused try), against `room-voice`'s twelve a minute. Docker Desktop hands the voice server a container's UDP from an address it picks (a Google or a GitHub one was seen), as C2 noted; C5's audit reads it with a packet capture.

**As built (C5, audit; 2026-11-19).** C5's second session audited the voice against ADR 031, ADR 024 and `/privacy` — readers by lens, the harness and the Docker stack with the `voice` profile driven live, a skeptic per finding — and fixed three blocking and four major findings, each with a test above that fails without it (ADR 031 "As built (C5, audit)"; every promise, its proof and its verdict in `~/elifba-shots/voice/c5/audit.md`, the observations under `audit/`).

- **Files.** `docker/caddy/Caddyfile` (a global `log default` filter; `@voice` on `/rtc/v1` and `/rtc/v1/validate`, matched on `{http.request.orig_uri.path}` too, `header_up -Cookie`; `@rtc` → 404), `docker/voice/livekit.yaml` (`logging.level: warn`, `room.enabled_codecs`), `docker/voice/livekit.dev.yaml` (`enabled_codecs`), `docker-compose.yml` (`x-logging` on every service), `docker/backup/elifba-backup.sh` (`--exclude-table-data` of the cache), `routes/console.php` and `App\Actions\Cache\PruneExpiredCache` (`cache:prune-expired`, every minute), `SweepRoomVoices` (`cutOnItsWay()`, `JOIN_SLACK_MS` = 2 000), `RoomVoiceServer::participants()` (`joined_at_ms`), `RoomVoiceUnreachable::report()` and `RoomVoiceNotCut::report()` (one line, no context), `RoomVoiceTicket::for()` (a row of its own room only), `tests/Support/FakeVoiceServer.php` (each participant's join moment from the app's clock); the tests are the guard table's rows marked "C5 (the audit)" and `scripts/docker-smoke.sh`'s new checks (40 without the profile, 70 with it, both green on the fixed files).
- **Seen live after the fixes.** The changed client of the audit, holding a ticket and a refreshed token from before the fact, was cut 5.9 s after a mute and 20.8 s after a leave (it had stayed 70.7 and 70.6 s; `audit/verify-harness/runs/`); the smoke stopped the voice server under a page's join and Caddy's two error lines held the path, no ticket, no join request, no browser or page, and the address as `x.y.0.0`; the voice server at `warn` wrote one line at start (the UDP buffer) and nothing of a join; a room made through the API offered audio codecs only. The rows of decision 31 the voice config touches were measured again in the C5 harness on loopback: mouth to ear 68 / 76 ms (n 108; it was 74 / 80), the host's mute to silence 54 / 110 ms (n 72; 48 / 61, on a laptop at load 4–14), and «Səsə qoşul» to hearing the room the same before and after the change, run interleaved — 190 / 226 ms with the old config and 188 / 215 ms with the new (n 36 each; the harness itself was 65 ms slower that day than for C5's first session, under both).
- **Not changed** (minor; `audit.md`): the voice server's one-minute JWT leeway, a call token scoped by kind, the sweep's first run after a restarted scheduler, a voice turned off and on admitting those who waited (decision 9), Docker's DNS asking the host's resolver for a stopped `voice`, the voice container's route out and privileges (the release's checklist), no Content-Security-Policy, and two sentences of ADR 031 decisions 14 and 17 that say less than the code does — the owner's to change.

**As built (C5, checklists; 2026-11-19).** C5's third session wrote [`docs/live-voice-checklists.md`](live-voice-checklists.md). The real-phone checklist is the owner's, with an iPhone (Safari) and an Android (Chrome): P1–P19 on the https try-out over Wi-Fi, P20–P23 on mobile data on a public instance — the try-out's LAN address is out of a mobile network's reach — each step with what to do, the interface's own words, the target and a blank per phone. The release checklist is any operator's: before (C5-O and "Accepted", the host's size, the two ports, the address, the key and the secret, `net.core.rmem_max`, a backup), turning on (the profile with the switch off, the smoke's voice checks read-only on the live host, the switch last), after (a first real room, `docker stats`, the logs read, `lk load-test`, a capture on the host), going back, and what is not built. Its first table maps every row of ADR 031 decision 31 and every drill of decision 26 to "measured (harness)" or to a phone's step; none is claimed for a phone. ⚑ marks the results that change the contract: decision 25's fallback (a phone's cues silent while its microphone is open), decision 26's half-open network, decision 4's TURN, decision 31's join on a mobile network, and an open microphone where the page promises none. No code changed. The phones, C5-O and the release are the owner's: ADR 031 stays "Proposed" and `ELIFBA_ROOMS_VOICE` off.

**As amended (voice on by default, 2026-11-19).** The owner, after the first day on elifba.io: «canlı səs defolt aktiv olsun, və bir dəfə icazə verildikdən sonra hərdəfə icazə istəməsin». ADR 031 "Amended 2026-11-19 (voice on by default)" has the measurements — WebKit forgets a page's microphone grant 10 minutes after its capture stops (1 minute for a request without a tap on an iPhone) and at every reload the person makes, and C4's `stopMicTrackOnMute: true` stopped the capture at every close — and decisions 35–43. The text of this subsection above reads as amended. What its build changes, with no migration, no new route, no new column and the state still `v: 5`:

- **The server.** `OpenRoom` takes the switch from `RoomController` (as `RoomState::for()` takes it) and sets `voice_at` and the host row's `voice_admitted_at`; `JoinRoom` stamps `voice_admitted_at` on a row without one while `voiceIsOn()` and no game runs; `SetRoomVoice` loses its refusal; `JoinRoomVoice` counts the voice rooms in use through `RoomVoiceServer::rooms()` (`ListRooms` with each room's `num_participants`, which `tests/Support/FakeVoiceServer` learns to answer) and throws `Rooms\Exceptions\RoomVoiceBusy` (503 `{status: "busy"}`) as `RoomVoiceUnreachable` renders its own; `config/elifba.php`'s comment on `rooms.voice.max_rooms` says "voice rooms in use".
- **The page.** `rooms/voice.ts`: `roomOptions()` with `stopMicTrackOnMute: false` and `stopLocalTrackOnUnpublish: false`; `VoiceLink` owns the one captured `LocalAudioTrack` (made at the first `setMic(true)`), mutes and unmutes it, publishes it again after `rejoin()`, and stops it in `leave()`, `dispose()`, at the host's mute and at a cut; the hidden page mutes it; a ticket outcome `busy`. `rooms/voice-view.ts`: who joins by itself (`joinsByItself()`), when the sentence opens by itself, the busy failure, the line to always allow, and the held voice's words (`rooms.voice.tap_to_hear` with the button's dot while `onAudio` says the browser holds the voice back). `rooms/voice-out.ts` (new, pure): the rooms this device left or declined, `localStorage` `elifba.rooms.voice.out`, the last twenty ids, every read and write in `try`/`catch`. `components/rooms/use-room-voice.ts`: the effect that joins by itself and opens the sentence by itself, the remembered rooms at «Səsdən çıx», «İndi yox» and «Səsə qoşul», the Permissions API's answer for the line. `components/rooms/voice-bar.tsx`: the line under «Mikrofonu aç», the busy failure, `rooms.voice.invite`'s new words; the comments that say "on the tap of «Səsə qoşul»" (`voice.ts`, `use-room-voice.ts`, `voice-bar.tsx`, `RoomsApartTest.php`) follow.
- **The words.** `rooms.voice.invite`, `rooms.voice.note.mic`, `welcome.rooms.names_voice` and `privacy.rooms.live_voice` change with the record (this commit); `rooms.voice.always_allow` and `rooms.voice.tap_to_hear` join "Words", `lang/` and `RoomsWordingTest`'s count with the build. `privacy.rooms.contact_voice` keeps «aparıcının açdığı canlı səsdə» until the owner's own question on that sentence is answered.
- **The tests** are the rows above marked "as amended 2026-11-19"; `PrivacyPageTest` and `WelcomePageTest` fail on this record's commit until the build changes the fragments they pin, since the sentences they quoted are no longer true.

**As amended (the head's microphone, 2026-11-19).** ADR 031 decision 44: in the voice the head's control is the microphone and a ⌄ for the panel (`VoiceButton`, `voice-bar.tsx`); `headMic(bar)` of `voice-view.ts` says what the head's microphone does, null out of the voice, where the head is the one button of decision 34. A head tap that brings the browser's question shows `rooms.voice.always_allow` as a note. No word, route, state or server changes.

**As amended (no «Səsə qoşul», 2026-11-19).** ADR 031 decision 45: the head is the microphone wherever the viewer is admitted — `headMic(bar)` is also `off` for a viewer out of the voice (`opening` while joining), whose tap joins and opens the microphone (`VoiceLink::join(agree, open)`); `rooms.voice.join` and `.invite_line` leave `lang/` and "Words" (`RoomsWordingTest`: 61).

**As amended (a reload keeps the microphone, 2026-11-19).** ADR 031 decision 46: `rooms/voice-reopen.ts` (`VoiceReopen`, `voiceReopen`, `REOPEN_MS` = 60 000) notes the room whose page went away with its microphone open (`use-room-voice.ts`: `VoiceLink`'s `onLeftOpen` when the library leaves with the page — it does so at `beforeunload`, before `pagehide` — the hidden page's close, and `beforeunload` / `pagehide`), forgets it when the page is shown again and takes it once at the next load of that room, whose join by itself then opens the microphone (`VoiceLink::join(false, true)`) — never for a muted standing. `privacy.rooms.live_voice`'s second paragraph says it.

**As built (voice on by default, 2026-11-19).** On `feat/rooms-voice-default`, as "As amended (voice on by default)" above lists, with ADR 031 "As built (voice on by default)" for what the code settled:

- **The server.** `OpenRoom::handle($host, $data, voice:)` (`RoomController::store` passes `RoomsArea::voice()`); `JoinRoom` stamps the admission of a row that comes in — new, or back after leaving — while `voiceIsOn()` and `runningGame()` is null; `SetRoomVoice` no longer counts; `RoomVoiceServer::rooms()` answers each voice room's name with its `num_participants` (the sweep reads the names); `JoinRoomVoice` makes one `ListRooms` per ticket before `CreateRoom` and throws `Rooms\Exceptions\RoomVoiceBusy` (`ShouldntReport`, 503 `{status: "busy"}`) while its room's voice room — of any epoch — is empty and `max_rooms` others are in use. A listing that does not answer is `RoomVoiceUnreachable`, named with the room's voice room.
- **The page.** `rooms/voice.ts`: `AUDIO_CAPTURE`; `VoiceLink` captures with the library's `createLocalAudioTrack()` at the first `setMic(true)`, publishes that track itself (`publishTrack`), closes it with `mute()`, publishes it again after `rejoin()` (open only if it was), and releases it (`unpublishTrack(track, true)`, `stop()`) at `leave()`, `dispose()`, a failure, a refused ticket, the host's mute and the browser's end of it — closing it first there, since the library recaptures an ended track that is still open; a ticket outcome `busy` fails at once, in a join and in a `rejoin()`; the `in` connection says `captured`; `onAudio` is told the library's `canPlaybackAudio` at every connection. `rooms/voice-view.ts`: `VoiceBrowser` (`held`, `question`) as `voiceView()`'s fourth argument, the `in` form's `held` and `alwaysAllow`, the trouble `busy`, and `mayJoinByItself()`, `joinsByItself()`, `opensSentenceByItself()`. `rooms/voice-out.ts` (new): `VoiceOut`, `voiceOut`, `deviceStorage()`. `components/rooms/use-room-voice.ts`: the join on the rise of `mayJoinByItself()`, the sentence once per page (closed by a game that begins, opened again after it), `dismissConsent` and `notNow` remembered, the Permissions API while the voice is drawn, the busy answer of a ticket. `components/rooms/voice-bar.tsx`: the line under «Mikrofonu aç», the held line and dot, the busy failure with «Yenidən cəhd et», the consent's every close as «İndi yox».
- **Seen in a browser** — Chrome on C4b's stack and Safari of an iOS 27.2 Simulator, outside the repository in `~/elifba-shots/voice/default/app/`: ADR 031 "As built (voice on by default)" has the checks and the numbers (one `getUserMedia` for open → close → open → close → open in Chrome; one microphone question per page in Safari; nothing of voice with the switch off). The first iOS run found a capture the browser ended being enabled again, which the library answers with a capture nobody tapped; fixed before the commit.
- **The tests** are the guard table's rows marked "as amended 2026-11-19", and `Tests\Support\Rooms` gains `open(…, voice:)` (false by default: a room opened with the switch off, which its host turns on) and `newcomer()`, a row that comes in while a game runs — the one way a newcomer still waits.

**Why.** Friends who play from different homes want to hear each other, and the only voice Elifba can vouch for is one that never leaves its own host. A second connection keeps the race exactly what it was: the voice can fail, lag or be switched off and no round knows. Facts the host wrote — on, admitted, muted — are rows under the room's lock, announced by the signal the pages already hear; everything live stays in the voice server and is forgotten there.

**Rejected.** A cloud voice service; phone to phone; a public STUN server; TURN over TLS in the first release; end-to-end encryption with a key from the same server; voice always on (on by default since 2026-11-19, ADR 031 decision 37); a microphone opened by anyone but its owner; a newcomer who listens before being admitted while a game runs; a mute that closes the voice room for all; a webhook; the screen as a loudspeaker or a watcher; push-to-talk; a nickname or an avatar for a speaker; recording; an age tick (ADR 031).

### The game frame (F2)

Designed in F1 (2026-11-19, ADR 032; variant A, «Başlıq», of three clickable prototypes — the private Artifact <https://claude.ai/artifact/Gp8hGfAjX7JntEfG78gXTX>, the files in `~/elifba-shots/game-frame/` — taken under the owner's standing rule); built by F2, which landed on 2026-11-19 ("As built (F2)" below), so the subsections above read as amended here where they place a room's page against the app's shell. The owner, on 2026-11-19: in a game the app's header and sidebar look unprofessional — a room's game should feel like the children's remote, a game of its own.

**Which pages.** `rooms/show`, in every state — the start screen, a round in each phase, the final, a player who left an open room, a closed room — has no app shell: `app.tsx`'s layout callback returns none for it, as for `rooms/screen`, and the page draws `RoomFrame` (`components/rooms/room-frame.tsx`). «Oyunlar» (`rooms/index`), «Keçmiş oyunlar» (`rooms/past`) and the entrance (`rooms/join`, `/oyun` and `/oyun/{code}`) keep `AppLayout` as the area's navigation; the frame begins after joining — «Qoşul», or from I2 a link opened by hand (ADR 033).

**The top bar.** One sticky line, 56px under `env(safe-area-inset-top)`: ‹ to «Oyunlar» (to «Keçmiş oyunlar» for a closed room), the page's `h1` — the round's title over the game and its difficulty in a round, the page's title elsewhere — and then «Oyun səsləri», the live voice's button and the code in a round, or the live voice's button and the host's «Otağın idarəsi» outside one. On a phone `RoundHead` keeps only its time row; the screen's is unchanged. The details are docs/frontend.md rule 15 "The game frame".

**Leaving.** ‹ never asks and leaves nothing: the account stays present in the room, the round goes on, and «Oyunlar» offers «Oyuna qayıt». «Oyundan çıx», «Otağı bağla» and «Oyunu bitir» are where §12 "Games first" put them, and «Oyunu bitir» never stands in the top bar (ADR 026 decision 2). A page that went to «Oyunlar» stops reading the state like any page that is closed; nothing on the server knows the frame.

**The surface** is the app's tokens in both themes; the frame has no colour of its own, so every room colour stays on the ground it was chosen for.

**Fit.** The phone's grid limit is `100svh − 21.25rem` (from `100svh − 28.5rem`): the top bar replaces the app's header one for one, the tab bar's 4rem comes back, and the round head's first row and its gap (3.25rem) move into the top bar. At 375 × 667 the grid has 327px — a 4 × 4 grid stays at its 19rem cap (cells about 71px), a 5 × 5 grid's cells are about 61px and a 6 × 6 grid's about 50px, so every question fits without scrolling, where the 5 × 5 and 6 × 6 scrolled before; at 390 × 844 the column's width decides (about 71, 67 and 55px). `RoomActionBar` sticks to the bottom of the viewport on every width and pads its own safe area; the voice's note keeps `env(safe-area-inset-top) + 4.25rem`, now under the frame's top bar; the taunt reaction stays a layer over the whole viewport; the live voice's button, the taunt fan and the voice's sheets take no height.

**Installed, laptop, motion, words.** The bars pad the safe areas and the viewport meta is unchanged; a phone page never asks for the Fullscreen API. From `lg` the same frame, no sidebar. The frame moves nothing of its own (ADR 025's round pop moves with the title). No string is added: ‹ says `nav.rooms` or `nav.rooms_past`, the round code button `rooms.show.code_chip`.

**Nothing else changes.** The state, the protocol, the clock, the channel, the points, the strip, the taunts, the sounds, the final, the live voice, `rooms/screen`, the children's pages and `RoomsApartTest`: ADR 032 "What does not change" lists them by record.

**Guard tests** (F2): a pure function of the page's name chooses the layout, under Vitest (`resources/js/lib/page-shell.test.ts`: "draws a room's page in its own game frame, never inside the app's shell (ADR 032)", "decides the shell of every room page by name") — `rooms/show` and `rooms/screen` without the shell, `rooms/index`, `rooms/past` and `rooms/join` with it, and no room page left to the default; `RoomsApartTest` keeps `room-frame.tsx` apart from the children's side; `recall-cells.tsx`'s limit is measured by F2's browser check ("What each step builds", "Game frame (F1–F2)"), not by a unit test.

**Why.** The shell is for moving between the area's pages; inside a game there is one page and one thing to do. The remote's single line is the precedent the owner named, and the screen's top bar already has the room's order. Keeping the tokens keeps every colour decision of ADRs 025–031 valid.

**Rejected.** B, «Səhnə» (a room surface of its own: every colour checked again, the choice's row gone from the start screen); C, «Dok» (the way out and the code beside the taps row and «Yenidən oyna», no heading in a round, the 6 × 6 cells at 44px); hiding only the tab bar in a round; the Fullscreen API; `/oyun/{code}` without the shell.

---

**As built (F2, 2026-11-19).** As above, with ADR 032 "As built (F2)": the shell is chosen by `pageShell()` (`resources/js/lib/page-shell.ts`), which `page-shell.test.ts` holds — `rooms/show` without the shell, every room page named; the round's title is `RoundTitle`, the page's `h1`; `RoomActionBar framed` is the frame's bar, while «Oyunlar» and the entrance keep it above the tab bar; the bar's ground is a layer of its own so the voice's `fixed` note is placed against the viewport. Measured in a harness outside the repository (`~/elifba-shots/game-frame/f2/`): every mechanic fits 375 × 667, 390 × 844 and an installed 375 × 647 with the voice on and the fan open, items ≥ 44px (6 × 6: 50, 55, 46px) and nothing covered; the host, a player and the screen played a game to its final, «Yenidən oyna» and a closed room in light and dark (`~/elifba-shots/game-frame/after/`).

### The invitation link (I2)

Designed in I1 (2026-11-19, ADR 033) under the owner's standing rule, before its code; I2 builds it. The owner, on 2026-11-19: the link should go to WhatsApp from beside the QR and the code; whoever opens it should land in the game without «Qoşul»; and someone with no account yet should get in from it easily. Nothing below loosens a rule of a room: every player is still an account with a confirmed address and the player role (ADR 020), a code still admits into one room and grants nothing else, a visitor still reads nothing of a room, nothing is typed in a room, and no page makes a third-party request.

**What is amended, and where the old text is.**

| Above                                                                                   | From | Reads                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| §1 "Accounts and roles" — the player group holds every room route                       | I2   | every one but the invitation's door (`GET`, `POST /oyun/qeydiyyat`): `rooms.enabled`, `guest`, no `auth`, no room read                                |
| §1 — Registration creates an account with no role                                       | I2   | the door, and the Google completion page while an invitation is under way, give the player role through `GrantRole`                                   |
| §7 "Agreements", "Google sign-in" step 3                                                | I2   | the player's sentence accepted there too, a tick of its own beside the privacy note's                                                                 |
| "The area and the switch" — An invited account                                          | I2   | a visitor who opened the link by hand comes to the door; every such path ends in the game                                                             |
| "Rooms and joining" — `GET /oyun/{code}` shows `rooms/join` or redirects a member       | I2   | opened by hand, or with the invitation under way for that code, it joins at once through `JoinRoom`; every other request gets the join page as before |
| "Games first" — the QR is `{APP_URL}/oyun/{code}`                                       | I2   | `{APP_URL}/oyun/{code}?t={tag}`, the shared link too                                                                                                  |
| "Games first" — the start screen's invitation, «Dostunu çağır», the round's code dialog | I2   | each gains the share row; the big screen does not                                                                                                     |
| "The game frame (F2)" — the frame begins after «Qoşul»                                  | I2   | after joining, by «Qoşul» or by a link opened by hand                                                                                                 |
| "Adults' privacy" — No contact: a code passes from person to person outside Elifba      | I2   | so does the link, sent by the person through WhatsApp or the system's sheet; Elifba still sends no invitation itself                                  |
| "Adults' privacy" — Notice                                                              | I2   | the section gains `privacy.rooms.share`, and `privacy.rooms.what` names the link and the newcomer's separate tick                                     |

**Sharing** (ADR 033 decisions 1–4). `ShareRow` (`components/rooms/room-code.tsx`) — two 44px `outline` buttons under the start screen's `InviteCard` and inside `CodeDialog` (the round's code chip, a player's «Dostunu çağır»), never on `rooms/screen`:

- «WhatsApp» (`MessageCircle`; `aria-label` `rooms.share.whatsapp_label`) is an `<a href="https://wa.me/?text=…" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">` whose text is `rooms.share.text` with `:url` = `join.url`, encoded whole: a link the person taps, not a request of the page — no WhatsApp script, widget, icon, font or image, no `fetch`, no logo or brand colour; `noreferrer` keeps the room page's address (its ULID) from WhatsApp. The message names nobody.
- The second button is «Paylaş» (`navigator.share({ text })`) on a touched device with a share sheet in a secure context, else «Linki kopyala» (`navigator.clipboard.writeText(join.url)`, then «Link kopyalandı» for 2 s), else nothing. `shareWay()` in `resources/js/rooms/share.ts` decides, pure.
- `/privacy` gains `privacy.rooms.share`: what the message holds, that WhatsApp sees it under its own rules, that its preview fetch sees only the general page tags, that the browser keeps the code in its session for an hour at most, and that the link dies with the room.

**The tag** (decision 5). `RoomJoinLink::of()`'s `url` is `{APP_URL}/oyun/{code}?t={tag}`, `RoomJoinLink::tag($room)` the first 6 lowercase hex characters of `hash_hmac('sha256', 'room-link:'.$room->id, APP_KEY)`, derived and stored nowhere. A `t` that is not the open room's tag gets the one refusal, `rooms.errors.unknown`, counted by `room-join`; no `t` is as before. It grants nothing; it keeps an old link from joining another room that drew the same code. The QR shows the same `url`; `scannedCode()` accepts it and visits `rooms.join` with the six digits.

**Opened by hand** (decision 6). `App\Support\RoomInvitation::openedByHand(Request)`: exactly `GET`; none of `Sec-Purpose`, `Purpose`, `X-Purpose`, `X-Moz`; no `X-Inertia`, not `expectsJson()`; `Sec-Fetch-Mode: navigate` and `Sec-Fetch-Dest: document`; and `Sec-Fetch-Site: none` (another app, the camera, the address bar) or `Sec-Fetch-User: ?1` (a tap on a web page). Anything else — a preview, a prefetch or prerender, `HEAD`, an `<img>`, a `fetch`, an iframe, a script's navigation without a tap, Inertia, JSON, a browser without fetch metadata — is not by hand.

**What `GET /oyun/{code}` answers** (decision 7), after the player group's middleware and `throttle:room-join` as before:

| The account and the request                                                     | Answer                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| unknown code, closed room, removed account, or a `t` that is not the room's tag | `/oyun` with `rooms.errors.unknown` (as before)                                                |
| the room's host                                                                 | the room, as they are (a link never changes whether the host plays)                            |
| a present player                                                                | the room (as before)                                                                           |
| opened by hand, or the invitation under way names this code                     | `JoinRoom`, then the room; `locked` or `full`: the join page with the reason, as after «Qoşul» |
| anything else                                                                   | page `rooms/join` and «Qoşul» (`POST /oyun/{code}`), as before                                 |

**The invitation under way** (decision 8). `App\Http\Middleware\RememberRoomInvitation` (`rooms.invitation`), on `GET /oyun/{code}` and ordered before `auth`, writes the session's `rooms.invitation` `{code, tag, until}` when the switch is on and the request is opened by hand — for a visitor, an account without a role, an unconfirmed one, one without the player role and a ready one alike. It reads no room. It lasts `elifba.rooms.invitation_minutes` (60), a newer open replaces it, a logout ends it. `GET /oyun/{code}` pulls it once when it names that code (and `t`, if given, is its tag) and then joins as if opened by hand — at the end of a login, the door, Google's completion, Settings → «Rollar», `/role`, the mail's link or the notice looking again. The intended address still routes the way; the invitation only proves the tap. A speculative, `HEAD` or JSON request never pulls it.

**The name** (decision 9). A player's start screen keeps «Qoşuldunuz», «:name kimi», and gains «Dəyiş» into Settings → Profil; in a round the joiner's side of the strip names them. No toast.

**The door** (decisions 10–13). `auth` sends a visitor of `rooms.join` whose session holds the invitation to `rooms.invited` (`redirectGuestsTo` → `RoomsArea::door()`), every other visitor to `/login`; the intended address is kept either way.

- `GET /oyun/qeydiyyat` (`RoomInvitedController::create`, page `rooms/invited` in the auth card) renders while the session holds an invitation, else redirects to `/login`; it reads no room and is the same for any code. `rooms.invited.title`, `rooms.invited.line` with «Daxil olun» to `/login`, «Google ilə davam et» first (`/auth/google/redirect?remember=`), «və ya e-poçt ilə», then the form.
- `POST /oyun/qeydiyyat` (`throttle:room-invited`, 30 a minute per IP) → `App\Actions\Fortify\CreateInvitedPlayer`: name (with «Oyunda adınız belə görünəcək.»), e-mail, one password with its eye (`Password::default()`, no `confirmed`), «Məni xatırla» on (ADR 017), `terms` and `agreement` accepted — two ticks, the privacy note's and the player's sentence; one transaction creates the account as `CreateNewUser` does and gives the player role through `GrantRole`. The controller fires `Registered`, logs in with the box and redirects to the intended address.
- Google: an existing account logs in and goes on (through Settings → «Rollar» if it lacks the role); a new one gets `auth/google-register` with `player: true` — the player's tick and «Hesab yarat və qoşul» — and `CreateUserFromGoogle($profile, $input, $invited)` gives the role through `GrantRole`.
- `verified` stays. The notice (`auth/verify-email`) gets `game` (an invitation under way, the switch on) and its line `rooms.invited.verify_line`, and for every account it reloads itself when shown again (`visibilitychange`), so a confirmed account goes on to the intended address.
- ADR 033 decision 15 is the alternative left to the owner: an unconfirmed account playing the one room it was invited to. Not built.

Routes (beside "Rooms and joining" and "Games first", which hold):

| Method and path                                | Middleware                                                         | Answer                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GET /oyun/{code}` (`rooms.join`)              | `rooms.invitation` before the player group's; `throttle:room-join` | the table above                                                                   |
| `GET /oyun/qeydiyyat` (`rooms.invited`)        | `rooms.enabled`, `guest`                                           | page `rooms/invited` while an invitation is under way, else `/login`              |
| `POST /oyun/qeydiyyat` (`rooms.invited.store`) | `rooms.enabled`, `guest`, `throttle:room-invited`                  | `CreateInvitedPlayer`; `Registered`; logged in; the intended address              |
| `GET`, `POST /auth/google/register`            | as §7 "Google sign-in"                                             | while an invitation is under way: the player's tick, the role through `GrantRole` |

**Steps** at 375 × 667 (ADR 033 decision 16): a signed-in player from 2 taps to 1; a newcomer with Google from 8 taps and 4 pages to 6 taps and 2; a newcomer by e-mail from 10 taps, 4 fields and 5 pages to 7 taps, 3 fields and 2 (the mailbox in both); a player signed out in the browser stays at 3 taps with a password and goes from 4 to 3 with Google.

**The switch.** With `elifba.rooms.enabled` off: `/oyun/{code}` and the door are 404, no invitation is written, `RoomsArea::door()` is null (a visitor goes to `/login`), the completion page and the notice say nothing of a game, and `/privacy`'s rooms section with `privacy.rooms.share` is not drawn.

**Guard tests.** ADR 033 "To build" lists them by file: `JoinRoomTest` (the opened-by-hand dataset, the invitation's one use and its minutes, the tag, the host), `RegistrationTest` (the door), `GoogleSignInTest`, `EmailVerificationTest`, `RoleAreasTest` ("keeps the invitation's door the only room route outside the player group"), `RoomsSwitchTest`, `RoomsApartTest`, `RoomPagesTest`, `RoomsWordingTest`, `TeacherIsolationTest`, `BearerReachTest`, `PrivacyPageTest`; Vitest `share.test.ts`, `page-shell.test.ts`, `scan.test.ts`.

**Why.** A link in a chat is how people already invite each other; WhatsApp's own `wa.me` address carries it without a request of ours. The person's tap is the consent «Qoşul» asked for, and the browser says whether there was one; where it cannot, today's page stays. An account remains the price of a name in a room, so the newcomer's path is shortened around it — one page, two ticks, the role in the same step, Google before the mailbox — rather than past it.

**Rejected.** A WhatsApp widget, logo or `whatsapp://` link; a share button on the big screen; Elifba sending invitations itself; a short-link service; joining on any `GET`; a script pressing «Qoşul»; a signed token per invitation; no tag, or codes never reused; a player without an account; `verified` relaxed (left to the owner, ADR 033 decision 15); the sentences merged or pre-ticked; the ticks before Google; a passwordless link or a typed code from the mail; `/register` with a mode for rooms (ADR 033 has the reasons).

**As built (I2, 2026-11-19).** As above, with ADR 033 "As built (I2)": `RoomInvitation` (`openedByHand`, `remember`, `pending`, `pull`, `tagOf`, `wellFormed`) reads no invitation while the switch is off; a link with a malformed tag is not remembered; `RoomsArea::door()` names the door only for the link whose code the invitation holds; `show()` takes the invitation before anything else, sends a host to the room for every request, and answers a locked or full room on open with page `rooms/join` and the reason in the same response, never a redirect. The start screen's QR stays 160px: with six players at 375 × 667 the choice's row ends 129px above «Başla». Seen in headless Chromium at 375 × 667, light and dark, az and en (`~/elifba-shots/invite/`): the WhatsApp link, copying, the sheet, no share row on the screen, no request off the app; a signed-in player in with the tap; a newcomer through the door and the mail without `/role`, in the same browser and, by the notice looking again, from another; a signed-out player and a teacher in; a preview's and a prefetch's fetch at the login. The real-phone matrix of ADR 033 "Seen on devices" is still the owner's.

### Board games (ADR 034)

A room may be opened for a board game instead of the round games: `rooms.board` (`RoomBoardGame`, `checkers`; null for the round games), written only by `OpenRoom`. «Oyunlar» lists «Şaşki» after the round games; `RoomPolicy::start` refuses a board room, and `startMatch` / `endMatch` are its host's.

- **Tables.** `room_matches` — the room, `position`, `board_game`, `white_player_id` and `black_player_id` (`room_players`), the position after the last move (`board`, 64 characters), `turn`, `ply`, `quiet_plies`, `last_path`, `started_ms`, and once over `ended_ms`, `result` (`w`, `b`, `draw` or null) and `ending` (`RoomMatchEnding`). `room_moves` — `event_id` (unique), the match, `ply` (unique per match), the player, `path` and `captured` (square numbers), `created_ms`. Both go with their room; neither holds a name or anything typed.
- **The judge.** `Rooms\Support\Checkers` alone (Russian draughts: flying kings, compulsory and continued capture, crowning mid-capture, captured pieces removed at the end of the move). TypeScript never computes a move: the page is sent the legal moves.
- **Writers.** `StartRoomMatch` (the host; two present players), `PlayRoomMove` (the player to move; `accepted`, `duplicate`, `late`, 422, 403), `EndRoomMatch` (a resignation, the host's end, and — from `LeaveRoom`, `RemoveRoomPlayer` and `CloseRoom` under the room's lock — a player gone or the room closed). Each raises the room's revision and signals `room.changed`, which still carries nothing else.
- **Reading.** `GET /rooms/{room}/match` (`watch`, `no-store`): `RoomMatchStateData`. The room page draws `CheckersRoom` and reads it again whenever the room's revision rises.
- **Clock, standing, taunts, screen, sound** (ADR 034 decisions 9–13). Five minutes a side (`elifba.rooms.checkers.clock_ms`), running on its turn only (`white_left_ms`, `black_left_ms`, `turn_since_ms`); a side out of time has lost, derived by `RoomMatch::settled()` on every read, never by a job. A win is `RoomPointsRule::MATCH_WIN` (3) and a draw `MATCH_DRAW` (1) in `RoomRanking::ofRoom()`. Taunts in a match are `room_match_taunts`, written only by `ThrowMatchTaunt`: free, at the opponent, after the cooldown, behind the host's switch; the match read carries them as a round game's state does (`received`, and `shown` for `?for=screen`). The screen draws `ScreenCheckers`; sound is the room's own files through `useMatchSound()`.

## What each step builds

- **A2 — Skeleton and CI gate.** `laravel new` with the React starter kit (built-in auth); `app/Domain/*` folders; `config/elifba.php`; `users.locale`; `lang/az.json` + `en.json` (+ framework PHP files) and the parity test with az as base; `SetLocale`; `QUEUE_CONNECTION=sync`, and `composer dev` running Vite + logs + `artisan serve` without the queue worker (Reverb joins it in A7, which installs Reverb); Inter loaded through the `local()` font provider, with no font CDN left from the starter kit; Pint, PHPStan 8 (Larastan), Pest, `tsc`, the kit's lint; `composer ci:check` and `.github/workflows/ci.yml` with the `ci` and `pgsql` jobs; README (az + en), LICENSE (MIT), CONTRIBUTING, `CLAUDE.md` with §11's rules, `docs/frontend.md` adapted from the precedent. Done when the login page opens in az and en and the gate is green.
- **A3 — Teachers, classes, students.** The `Language` minimum the classroom needs: `content/alphabet.json` (letters, vowels, look-alikes), `Alphabet`, `Az` (`upper`, `lower`, `title`, `normalize`) and `AzCollator`, with their tests; `ext-intl` required. `SchoolClass`, `Student`, the Classroom actions, paste-a-list with preview (`NameListParser`, §7: strips numbering, bullets, number columns, surnames and patronymics, reads surname-first registers, adds a surname initial only to tell two equal first names apart, flags duplicates), avatars auto-assigned from `content/avatars.json` (A3 creates it with keys only, read by a small `Content\Support\Avatars`; until A5 adds the pictures an avatar renders as a coloured circle), archive / restore / delete, the 32 learned-letter toggles, `BelongsToOwner` scoped binding, policies, the route-enumerating isolation test; the registration checkbox (`users.terms_accepted_at`); `/privacy` with `elifba.operator.*` and the parent note.
- **A4 — Language core and content.** `Language` extended from A3's minimum: letter names and confusables in `alphabet.json`, `Syllabifier` (with `shape()`), `Ordinal`, with tests; `content/` structure, `Content\Enums` (`Mechanic` moved here from Play, §2), `Content\Data` (pack, word, sentence, level, prompt template, credit), `ContentRepository`, `ContentValidator` with one broken fixture per rule, `content:validate` / `content:cache` / `content:clear` in `optimize`, the `public/content` link (`storage:link` in `composer setup`), `credits.json` with its coverage check; `alphabet.json`, `levels.json`, `prompts.json`; six packs with ≥ 20 words and ≥ 10 sentences each (session 1: `fruit`; session 2: the other five); `content/tests/syllables.json` with ≥ 100 words.
- **A5 — Images and sounds.** Kenney avatars and sounds; OpenMoji word pictures (own folder); own SVGs in OpenMoji's style for the words OpenMoji lacks, the symbols pack (flag rules of §8) and the cities (a map with a marker); Andika and Inter self-hosted; every asset in `content/credits.json`, the generated `CREDITS.md` and `/credits`; the validator's picture check switched on. Every word of every pack has a picture; no asset without a license.
- **A6 — Board and the first mechanic.** `GameSession`, `GameRound`, `GameAnswer`, `GameSetupData`, `StartGame` with the `find` builder, the round contract (DTOs, zod schemas in `contract.ts`, fixtures, both fixture tests; `zod` added), `RoundJudge` + `engine.ts` with shared cases (Vitest through `vp test` joins the gate), `ClaimBoard`, `RecordBoardEvents` (`/sync`), `PointsRule`, `SchoolCalendar`, `TransitionGameSession`, the board page (Phaser scene for `find`, `balloon` style, `burst` effect, sounds (Kenney CC0 with credits entries if A5 has not landed), full screen, wake lock, outbox, reload/resume), touch and mouse play. Built in two sessions: the server side first (the tables, the contract and its fixtures, the judge on both sides, `Playable`, the `find` builder, the actions and the board endpoints, `routes/play.php`, with minimal `games/setup` and `games/board` pages), then A6.2, the board itself (Phaser, outbox, reload/resume, the setup picker).
- **A7 — Phone remote and guest board.** `PairingCode`, `IssueJoinCode`, the pairing card (QR + code), `/pult` and `/pult/{code}`; the guest board (`guest_boards`, `/lovhe`, `OpenGuestBoard`, `ClaimGuestBoard`, `ShowGameOnGuestBoard`, `CloseGuestBoard`, `PruneGuestBoards`, the `board` guard, the game setup on the phone); the channel callback and its tests (only the owner's session or the guest board showing that game can subscribe; a bearer cannot reach any other game or page), `live.ts` with the client-event protocol v1, the remote page (items as big buttons, the answer highlighted, student grid, next, pause, skip, end), acks, retries, pings, reconnect banners, `?debug=1` latency, `session.status` broadcast, Reverb installed and added to `composer dev`, `composer dev:lan`. Measured p95 `ack_ms` ≤ 300 ms.
- **A8 — Syllable train, word and sentence games.** Builders and scenes for `build_syllable`, `build_word`, `build_sentence`, with reusable tiles (`nə-nə`); `wagon` and `frame` slot styles; the remaining item styles (`wagon`, `card`, `bubble`) and the `sparkle` effect; every mechanic × every pack, all driven by the remote.
- **A9 — Points and rankings.** `Rankings\Support\ClassRanking` (daily, weekly) and `DenseRank`, `Rankings\Enums\Period`, `Rankings\Data\PeriodData` (the shared `?period&date` query, its labels and the windows before and after) and `RankedStudentData`; `Language\Support\DateLabel` and `Ordinal::label()` for English places; the rankings page `/classes/{class}/rankings` (`routes/rankings.php`, `RankingController`, `rankings/show`, `components/rankings`) with day / week, previous / next / today, linked from the class page; tests for midnight Baku, Sunday→Monday, dense ties in Azerbaijani order, archived and deleted students, whole-class rounds, other classes, invalid and future dates, both locales and isolation.
- **A10 — Podium and share card.** The podium page (projector-friendly, confetti), the 1080×1920 card, `modern-screenshot` capture, Web Share with files and the download fallback, the §6 content list enforced by a test of the props the card receives.
- **A11 — Docker and release prep.** Dockerfile on `serversideup/php`, `docker-compose.yml` (proxy, app, reverb, scheduler, db with backups), `.env.production.example` (with `ELIFBA_OPERATOR_NAME` / `ELIFBA_OPERATOR_EMAIL`), install section in both READMEs, issue and PR templates, screenshots. `docker compose up` on a clean machine serves the app with working Reverb.

### Roles and parents (R1–R7)

R1 wrote ADRs 010–013 and the R-marked text of this document; it changed no code. Each step below changes the contract first if it has to depart from it, runs `composer ci:check`, and is done only when its named tests exist and pass on SQLite and PostgreSQL.

- **R2 — Roles and the role choice.**
    - _Scope._ `spatie/laravel-permission` and everything in §1 "Accounts and roles" and §7 "Agreements": the two roles, `/role`, `role.chosen`, the four route groups, `/start`, the second role, the area switch, existing accounts as teachers, the navigation per area. No parent page yet: until R3, `/start` sends a parent to `/children`, which R2 ships as a plain placeholder page in the parent area.
    - _Files._ `composer.json`; `config/permission.php`; migrations `create_permission_tables` (the package's, published) and `add_roles_to_users` (`area`, `teacher_agreed_at`, `parent_agreed_at`; the two `roles` rows; every existing account a teacher); `app/Enums/Role.php`; `app/Models/User.php` (`HasRoles`, `area()`); `app/Actions/Roles/{GrantRole,SwitchArea}.php`; `app/Data/RoleChoiceData.php`; `app/Http/Middleware/EnsureRoleChosen.php`; `app/Http/Controllers/{RoleController,StartController,AreaController}.php`, `Settings/RolesController.php`; `bootstrap/app.php` (aliases `role`, `role.chosen`; `redirectUsersTo`; the 403 → `/start` rule); `app/Providers/AppServiceProvider.php` (morph alias); `config/fortify.php` (`home`); every `routes/*.php` (the groups); `app/Http/Middleware/HandleInertiaRequests.php` (`auth.roles`, `auth.area`); `app/Actions/Fortify/{CreateNewUser,CreateUserFromGoogle}.php` and `app/Http/Controllers/Auth/GoogleController.php` (the redirect; the `terms` text); `database/factories/UserFactory.php`; `resources/js/pages/auth/{choose-role,register,google-register}.tsx`, `pages/settings/roles.tsx`, `components/app/nav-areas.ts`, the sidebar, bottom bar and user menu; `lang/az.json`, `lang/en.json`; `php artisan wayfinder:generate --with-form`.
    - _Tests._ `tests/Feature/Auth/RoleChoiceTest.php` ("sends a new account to the role choice", "sends an account that signed up with Google to the role choice", "grants the chosen role and stamps its agreement", "refuses a role without its agreement", "passes an account that already has a role on to its home"); `tests/Feature/Auth/RoleAreasTest.php` ("puts every authenticated route in one area", "redirects a parent's page visit to the teacher area home", "refuses a parent's write to the teacher area", "answers 404 for another account's class whatever the role", "answers 409 to a JSON request without a role", "keeps the profile reachable without a role", "shares the roles and the area with every page"); `tests/Feature/Auth/StartTest.php` ("lands by role"); `tests/Feature/Auth/RoleMigrationTest.php` ("makes every existing account a teacher with its agreement"); `tests/Feature/Settings/RolesTest.php` ("adds the second role with its agreement", "switches the area", "refuses an area the account has no role for", "never removes a role"); `tests/Feature/Auth/RolesTableTest.php` ("holds exactly the roles of the enum"); and, updated, `RegistrationTest`, `GoogleSignInTest`, `ProfileUpdateTest` ("deleting the account removes its roles"), `BearerReachTest`, `TeacherIsolationTest`, `TranslationsTest`.
    - _Finish line._ An account without a role sees only the role choice; a parent cannot enter a teacher route; every existing account is a teacher; PHPStan level 8 passes with no ignore and no baseline.
- **R3 — Children and home games (server), and `/privacy`.**
    - _Scope._ The `Family` domain, `children`, Play generalised by `GamePlayers` (§1, §2 "Game setup", §4 "At home"), stars (§5), deletion (§7), the parent section of `/privacy`, the trait rename. Pages only as far as the routes need (R4 designs them): the placeholder of R2 becomes a working, plain `children/index` and `children/show`.
    - _Files._ `app/Domain/Family/**`; migrations `create_children_table`, `add_child_to_games` (nullable `school_class_id` and `child_id` on `game_sessions` and `game_rounds`, the two indexes); `config/elifba.php` (`children.max`); `app/Models/Concerns/BelongsToOwner.php` (renamed, four models); `app/Domain/Play/Data/{GamePlayers,GameSetupData,GuestBoardGameData}.php`, `Play/Actions/{StartGame,RecordBoardEvents}.php`, `Play/Models/{GameSession,GameRound}.php`, `Play/Support/{GameSetupOptions,PultChoices,BoardProps,StartableGame}.php`, `Play/Events/RoundSolved.php`; `app/Domain/Rankings/{Data/ChildStarsData,Support/ChildStars}.php`; `routes/family.php`, `routes/play.php`; `app/Http/Controllers/Family/*`, `Play/GameController.php`; `PrivacyController` and `resources/js/pages/privacy.tsx`; factories; `lang/*`; `docs/glossary.md` if a name changes.
    - _Tests._ `tests/Feature/Family/ChildActionsTest.php` ("adds a child with the next avatar", "refuses a name the parent already uses, compared with Azerbaijani casing", "refuses an eleventh child", "refuses a surname initial", "stores the child's learned letters", "deletes a child with their games, rounds and answers"); `tests/Feature/Play/HomeGameTest.php` ("starts a game for a child from the child's learned letters", "credits every solved round to the game's child, whatever student id the board sends", "stores no student on a home round or answer", "keeps a home round out of every class ranking and podium", "sends a home game to a guest board from the parent's phone", "lets the bearer reach only the home game its board shows"); `tests/Feature/Play/GamePlayersMigrationTest.php` ("keeps every existing game with its class"); `tests/Feature/Rankings/ChildStarsTest.php` ("sums today, the week and all across Baku midnight and Monday", "keeps siblings' stars apart", "counts nothing of a deleted child"); `TeacherIsolationTest` with `{child}` in `ISOLATED_PARAMETERS` and a case for every child route; `RoleAreasTest` ("refuses a teacher without the parent role on every child route") and `BearerReachTest` with the new routes; `ChannelAuthTest` ("authorizes the owner of a home game and the board that shows it, and nobody else"); `AbandonAndPruneTest` ("prunes old home games"); `PrivacyPageTest` ("has the section for parents in both languages"); the existing Play and Rankings suites unchanged in what they assert for classes.
    - _Finish line._ A parent adds a child, starts a game for them, plays it by touch and sees the stars; another parent's child is a 404; `composer ci:check` passes and the `pgsql` job is green.
    - _As built (2026-11-11)._ Where the text above leaves a choice, R3 made it so:
        - **Routes.** `routes/family.php` (parent area): `GET|POST /children` (`children.index`, `children.store`), `PUT /children/order` (`children.order`, the ids in the body, checked by `ReorderChildren`), `GET|PATCH|DELETE /children/{child}` (`children.show`, `children.update`, `children.destroy`), `PUT /children/{child}/learned-letters`; in `routes/play.php`, also in the parent area, `GET /children/{child}/games/create` and `POST /children/{child}/games` (`GameController::createForChild`, `storeForChild`, beside `create` and `store` for a class). `{child}` is a ULID (`whereUlid`), bound by `BelongsToOwner` and checked by `ChildPolicy` (`view`, `update`, `delete`).
        - **Names in Play.** `GamePlayers` also has `isHome()` and `columns()` (the `school_class_id` and `child_id` a game and its rounds store); `GameSetupOptions::forClass()` became `GameSetupOptions::for(GamePlayers)`, `GameSession::teacher()` became `owner()`, and `GameSession::child()` was added. `GameSetupData::learned()` stays as a delegate of `GamePlayers::learned()`.
        - **The lock.** `Family\Support\FamilyLock` takes the lock on the parent's `users` row that `Content\Support\TeacherLock` takes for teacher words, and reads the parent's names as `DisplayName::key()`.
        - **The name rule.** `new DisplayName(initial: false, message: 'children.errors.invalid_name')`: the student's shape without the trailing initial, and no word of one letter (an initial without its dot). `ChildData`'s `avatar` is optional: `AddChild` picks the next unused one, `UpdateChild` keeps the current one.
        - **Stars on the board.** `BoardProps.child.stars` is summed by `BoardProps` itself over the child's rounds of other games: Play cannot use `Rankings`, which comes after it.
        - **Page props.** `children/index`: `children[] {id, display_name, avatar, stars_today}` in the parent's order, `avatars`, `limits.max`. `children/show`: `child {id, display_name, avatar, learned_letters}`, `stars` (`ChildStarsData`: `today`, `week`, `total`, `days[] {date, label, stars}`, oldest first), `alphabet`, `avatars`, `retentionDays`. `games/setup`: `schoolClass` or `child` (the other null). `/pult` games carry `schoolClass` or `child` `{id, display_name}`; the guest board's choices add `children` and `setup.children`. `/privacy` gets `childrenMax`; the parent section is `privacy.home.*`. The pages are plain (R4 designs them); the class page's `LearnedLetters` takes the URL it saves to, so the child's page reuses it.
        - **Guest board.** `GuestBoardGameData` takes exactly one of `game`, `class` and `child`; a new game's `GamePlayers` is built from the account's own class or child.
        - **The migration on SQLite** rebuilds `game_sessions` and `game_rounds` with the foreign keys switched off; that switch is ignored inside a transaction (where a dropped table would cascade to its rounds), so `GamePlayersMigrationTest` runs the migration on an in-memory database of its own on SQLite, and inside the test's transaction on PostgreSQL.

No server rule changes.

The name and the avatar are edited in a dialog; the order is changed on demand («Sıranı dəyiş»), not beside every card.
        - **Pairing at home** is decided in one place, `pairingOnLoad()` in `lib/game-players.ts`, which `BoardScreen` passes to `LiveBoard` (`pairing`); «Pultu qoş» also sits on a home board's start screen. `HomeBoardTest` checks the server side (the board page leaves the join code alone and «Pultu qoş» still pairs), `lib/game-players.test.ts` the decision.
        - **The result's total** is `child.stars` + the game's points as the board holds them (they start from `game.points`, so a reload mid-game counts nothing twice).
        - **Picture group first** was `levelsInOrder()` in `lib/setup-levels.ts` (since frontend.md rule 13, `unitGroups()` in `lib/setup-choices.ts`): at home every level whose unit is not one of the four read units goes first; until R5 there is none, so the order is unchanged.
