- **R5 — Picture rounds and the counting round.**
    - _Scope._ ADR 012 in code: `Unit::Picture`, `Unit::Number`, `Unit::reads()`, `Mechanic::Count`, `ItemStyle::Picture`, pack `units`, level `numbers`, `prompt.count`, the builders, the scene, the phone. And the content the animals and the fruit need to play it all: `units` in the six packs (`picture` and `number` for those two), the four levels `pictures-easy`, `pictures`, `numbers-5`, `numbers-10`, and the five templates. No new pack and no new asset: those are R6.
    - _Files._ `app/Domain/Content/Enums/{Unit,Mechanic,ItemStyle}.php`, `Content/Data/{PackData,LevelData,PromptTemplateData}.php`, `Content/Support/{ContentValidator,ContentRepository}.php`; `content/packs/*.json` (`units`, version + 1), `content/levels.json`, `content/prompts.json`; `tests/Fixtures/content/**` and `broken.json`; `app/Domain/Play/Data/{RoundData,PromptData,GameSetupData}.php`, `Play/Support/{Playable,GameSetupOptions}.php`, `Play/Support/Mechanics/{AnyRoundBuilder,FindRoundBuilder,PopRoundBuilder,CountRoundBuilder,RoundParts}.php`; `resources/js/game/{contract,stage,answers,figures}.ts`, `scenes/{count,layouts,find}.ts`, `styles/{picture,registry}.ts`; `components/games/{remote-items,remote-question,game-setup-form}.tsx`; `tests/Fixtures/contract/*` (every round and state fixture gains `count`; three new rounds; `judge.json`), `tests/Fixtures/playable.json`; `lang/*`.
    - _Tests._ `tests/Unit/Play/PictureRoundsTest.php` ("gives every item its picture and the prompt none", "ignores the learned letters", "never takes a teacher word", "leaves out a word the owner hid", "never asks the same picture twice in a row"); `tests/Unit/Play/NumberRoundsTest.php` ("takes the nearest numbers as decoys", "stays inside the level's range", "gives a number no picture in the animal and fruit styles" — the last in `figures.test.ts`); `tests/Unit/Play/CountRoundBuilderTest.php` ("shows the picture N times and never the number", "solves with the one item that says N", "deals every number equally often"); `ContractTest` ("round-trips every fixture" with the new ones, "reads a round stored before prompt.count"); `contract.test.ts`; `RoundJudgeTest` and `engine.test.ts` on the new `judge.json` case; `PlayableTest` and `styles/registry.test.ts` ("draws the picture style", "fits ten pictures in the question band"); `ContentValidatorTest` (a broken fixture for each new rule: `units`, the picture and number bars, `numbers`, a template for a unit no level has); `GameSetupTest` ("starts every picture and number level in animals and fruit for a class with one learned letter", "starts a counting game in animals and in fruit", "does not offer a level the pack does not list", "refuses it with pack_level"); `HomeGameTest` ("starts a picture game and a counting game for a child"); `SyncTest` ("gives a count round its points"); `answers.test.ts` (the phone's picture and count rounds).
    - _Finish line._ A picture game and a counting game start with the animals and the fruit, for a class and for a child, and play on the board and from the phone (seen in a browser); `round.find-picture.json` and `round.count.json` round-trip on both sides; `composer ci:check` and `vp test` pass.
    - _As built (2026-11-11)._ Where the text above leaves a choice, R5 made it so:
        - **Content.** Every pack got `units` after `title` and its `version` + 1 (animals and fruit 5, the others 4). The level titles «1-dən 5-ə» and «Şəkillər: asan» hold digits and a colon, which a title may now hold (`ContentValidator::TITLE_SIGNS`); a prompt still may not. The bar of 20 words and 10 sentences says "a pack of the reading units". A number level's range needs `items` + 1 numbers. `PackData::pictured()` lists the words with a picture (what a picture level names and a `count` round counts) and `PackData::lists()` whether a unit is listed; `LevelData::numberKey()` writes `number:{n}`.
        - **Builders.** The number decoys are `RoundParts::nearest()`, shared by `AnyRoundBuilder` and `CountRoundBuilder`. A number shortage, which valid content never reaches (the validator asks for `items` + 1 numbers), answers `too_few_numbers`; a picture shortage `too_few_pictures` with the counts (`:count` is the level's `items`, at least 2; for `count`, 2 things to count). A picture item's word and a number are never cased: `GameSetupData::fromArray()` stores `lower` at a level that does not read, whatever `letter_case` came.
        - **Contract.** `PromptData::fromArray()` reads a missing `count` as null (`StoredPromptArray`), so a snapshot stored before R5 loads; `RoundData::toArray()` always writes the key. `GameRound::$payload` is typed `StoredRoundPayload`.
        - **Board.** `styleOfUnit()` (`styles/registry.ts`, passed to the scenes by `stage.ts` as `styleOf`) gives a round of the picture unit the `picture` style. The find scene loads a round's pictures (`BoardScene::loadPictures()`, as figures, `figureKey()`) before its tiles come in, so a pack without figures (R6's colours) draws them too. `scenes/count.ts` extends the find scene (`itemLayout()`, `ask()`, `answered()`): `COUNT_LAYOUT` puts the question band at 24–572 and the numbers in build_syllable's tile band (600–1066); `countPictures()` gives 220 px pictures in rows of five that never shrink with N, centred when there are up to five, a ten-frame from six. A `picture` tile is at most 480 px, 0.84 of its column.
        - **Phone.** `remote-question.tsx` shows the picture and «× N» above a count round's number buttons; a picture item's button shows a larger picture with the word in `text-2xl`.
        - **Setup form.** (Replaced by frontend.md rule 13 and `lib/setup-choices.ts`.) `levelGroups()` and `readsUnit()` (`lib/setup-levels.ts`) grouped the levels; the group headings show only when both groups have a level for the chosen pack and game. The form's default is the first startable game in its own level order, so at home a picture level is chosen first. `pop` is labelled `games.mechanics.pop_say` while a picture or number level is chosen; the letter case and its hidden field are left out there.
        - **The welcome page** lists `Playable::mechanics()`, so «Say» appears there with its own line, `welcome.get.mechanic.count`; nothing else on the page changed (R7 rewrites it).
- **R6 — The colours and numbers packs.**
    - _Scope._ Data only, the proof of ADR 012's "content is data": `content/packs/colors.json` (`units: ["picture"]`, item style `picture`, the eleven colours of §3 "Units", each an own SVG of the same plain shape in that colour) and `content/packs/numbers.json` (`units: ["number"]`, at least eight things to count with their pictures); credits. No level, template, PHP or TypeScript changes — only pinned lists in tests; if the validator or a builder lacks something, the step stops and reports it.
    - _Files._ `content/packs/{colors,numbers}.json`, `content/assets/{colors,numbers}/*.svg`, `content/credits.json`, `CREDITS.md` (generated); tests that pin the packs.
    - _Tests._ `ContentValidatorTest` over the real content (the two packs, their assets, credits and SVG safety); `ContentRepositoryTest` ("lists eight packs"); `GameSetupTest` ("starts every picture and number level in every pack that lists its unit", a dataset over the real content, for a class and for a child); `CreditsDocumentTest` and the `CREDITS.md` freshness test; `php artisan content:validate`.
    - _Finish line._ Colours, digits and counting start and play for both roles; every new asset has a credits entry with an allowed license; checked in a browser on the board and the phone.
    - _As built (2026-11-11)._ No PHP, TypeScript, level or template changed; the validator and the builders took both packs as they are. Where the text above leaves a choice, R6 made it so:
        - **`colors`** (version 1): the shape is a circle (r 28 on the 72 grid, the black outline of §8), the same bytes in every file but the fill — `ContentRepositoryTest` checks it. The fills, all from §8's palette: qırmızı `#D22F27`, sarı `#FCEA2B`, yaşıl `#5C9E31`, göy `#1E50A0`, narıncı `#E27022`, bənövşəyi `#8967AA`, çəhrayı `#FFA7C0`, qəhvəyi `#6A462F`, qara `#000000`, ağ `#FFFFFF`, boz `#9B9B9A`. They were chosen among the palette's shades to keep the closest pair as far apart as possible: every pair is at least ΔE2000 19.5 on screen (qırmızı–narıncı) and 17.3 on a washed-out projector (ağ–boz; simulated with 25 % lift towards white and 30 % less saturation). The pack's own palette is one dark grey (`#3F3F3F`), so every tile is the same light grey and only the circle's colour differs; white stays visible by its outline. Effect `burst`, hit sound `pop`.
        - **`numbers`** (version 1): item style `bubble`, effect `burst`, four palette colours; ten things to count, each an own SVG under `content/assets/numbers/` — ulduz, ürək, top, gül, balıq, kəpənək, yarpaq, göbələk, bulud, şar. Simple flat subjects read best when the count scene draws one ten times at 220 px, and the step named own art, so none is an OpenMoji file although emoji exist for most (§3 "Pictures" prefers OpenMoji for a word's picture; changing one later is one path in the pack).
        - **Credits:** two MIT entries, `content/assets/colors/*` and `content/assets/numbers/*`, author "Elifba contributors"; `CREDITS.md` regenerated.
        - **Tests:** `ContentRepositoryTest` ("ships exactly the eight built-in packs", "ships the eleven colours of ADR 012 as one plain shape each, without «mavi»", "ships things to count with a picture each and no digit in the numbers pack", the units and styles of both); `GameSetupTest` ("starts every picture and number level in every pack that lists its unit", a dataset over `content/packs/*.json` for a class and for a child; the option counts; `pack_level` for the colours and the numbers at levels they do not list); `BuildRoundsTest` and `PlayableTest` loop over the packs that list a level's unit.
- **R7 — The landing page and the READMEs.**
    - _Scope._ A section for parents on the welcome page (frontend.md rule 10) and in `README.md` and `README.az.md`: what a parent does, the picture games, that there is no ranking at home. Screenshots or a short demo of a home game.
    - _Files._ `resources/js/pages/welcome.tsx`, `components/welcome/*`, `WelcomeController` (a sample picture round, if the wall shows one: `SampleRounds`), `README.md`, `README.az.md`, `lang/*`, `docs/screenshots/`.

    - _Finish line._ The landing page and both READMEs say what Elifba is for a parent; no page makes a third-party request; `composer ci:check` passes.
    - _As built (2026-11-11)._ The wall at the top did not change: it still plays `find` letter rounds (`SampleRounds` untouched), and the parent section shows a home game as screenshots instead. Where the text above leaves a choice, R7 made it so:
        - **The page** (frontend.md rule 10): «Evdə, uşağınızla» after «Nə əldə edirsiniz» and before the privacy part, with a link to it under the hero's actions. One new prop, `picturePacks` (the keys of the packs that list a unit nobody reads: today `animals`, `colors`, `fruit`, `numbers`). A signed-in account's actions go through `/start` and are named by its area (`welcome.hero.{classes,children,start}`).
        - **Pictures:** `resources/images/welcome/home.webp` (the board of an `animals` `find` round at `pictures-easy`, «zürafə» hansıdır?) and `home-remote.webp` (the parent's remote), from a scratch database with an invented parent and children (Nərmin, Tural, Ayla). The READMEs show `docs/screenshots/{role,children,child,home-board,home-remote}.png` from the same run.
        - **READMEs:** a section «Evdə, valideynlər üçün» / "At home, for parents" after "What it does", and the first steps of "What it does" and "Develop" now say that an account chooses its role after registering.
        - **Tests:** `WelcomePageTest` ("has the section for parents in both languages", "shows a signed-in parent the same page, with the parent area"); `LandingTest` ("makes no third-party request from the welcome page": every `src`/`href` the server's HTML loads is relative or on the app's host, and the page's TSX names no outside address); `TranslationsTest`.

### Adults' rooms (B1–B7)

B1 wrote ADRs 019–024, §12 and the B-marked text of this document; it changed no code. The owner reads the design before B2 starts (roadmap step B1-O) and switches the area on after B7 (B7-O). Each step below changes the contract first if it has to depart from it, runs `composer ci:check`, keeps `ELIFBA_ROOMS` off wherever it is not testing rooms, and is done only when its named tests exist and pass on SQLite and PostgreSQL. No step changes a file of `Classroom`, `Family`, `Play` or `Rankings`, a children's page, or the content outside `content/rooms/` and `content/assets/rooms/`, except the lines named below.

- **B2 — The area, the switch, rooms and joining.**
    - _Scope._ §12 "The area and the switch" and "Rooms and joining", and the B2 lines of §1 and §7: the role `player` with its sentence, the switch, the fifth route group and the play settings' move to the shared group; the `Rooms` domain with `rooms` and `room_players`, `RoomCode`, the ten room actions, `{room}` and `{member}`, `RoomPolicy`, the limiters, the two scheduled commands; the room channel and `room.changed`; `GET /rooms/{room}/state` for a room without a game (`room`, `me`, `players`, an empty `ranking`); the invited account's way in. Pages only as far as the routes need them, plain (B4 designs them): `rooms/index`, `rooms/join`, `rooms/show` with the lobby (the code, the players, lock, remove, leave, close). No game yet.
